# Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah

ברטנורא על משנה סוטה

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah)

חיבור התשתית הקדום של חז״ל, המתעד מגוון דעות הלכתיות ממסורת התורה שבעל פה. נחתם בשנת 200 לספירה לערך.

16th-century commentary printed in most editions of the Mishnah, including summaries of talmudic discussions and legal conclusions.

Sections: Chapter-Mishnah-Comment

Location: Mishnah/Rishonim on Mishnah/Bartenura/Seder Nashim

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (En): Bartenura on Mishnah, trans. by Rabbi Robert Alpert, 2020 (Segments: 310)


## Chapter 1


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:1:1)


**הַמְּקַנֵּא לְאִשְׁתּוֹ.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּהַמְּקַנֵּא מַשְׁמַע דִּיעֲבַד אִין לְכַתְּחִלָּה לֹא, הַהֲלָכָה הִיא שֶׁחַיָּב אָדָם לְקַנְאוֹת לְאִשְׁתּוֹ אִם הִיא צְרִיכָה לְכָךְ. וּלְקַמָּן מְפָרֵשׁ כֵּיצַד מְקַנֵּא:


המקנא לאשתו – even though that one suspects [one’s wife], which implies, post-facto, yes, and ab initio, no, the Halakha is that a man is obligated to be jealous over one’s wife if she is in need of this, and further on (Mishnah 2), it (i.e., the Mishnah) explains how one suspects [one’s wife].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:1:2)


**מְקַנֵּא לָהּ עַל פִּי שְׁנַיִם.** אִם בָּא לְהַשְׁקוֹתָהּ מֵי הַמָּרִים, צָרִיךְ לְהָבִיא שְׁנֵי עֵדִים וְלוֹמַר לָהּ בִּפְנֵיהֶם אַל תִּסָּתְרִי עִם אִישׁ פְּלוֹנִי. וְאִם לֹא קִנֵּא לָהּ בִּפְנֵי שְׁנַיִם, אֵינָהּ נֶאֱסֶרֶת עָלָיו בִּסְתִירָה זוֹ וְאֵינוֹ מַשְׁקֶה אוֹתָהּ:


מקנא לה על פי שנים – if he comes to cause her to drink the bitter waters, he must bring two witnesses, and to say to her in their presence, “do not hide yourself with a certain man,” but if he didn’t accuse her of unfaithfulness before two [witnesses], she is not prohibited to him through her retirement with this man under suspicious circumstances, and he doesn’t cause her to drink [the bitter waters].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:1:3)


**עַל פִּי עֵד אֶחָד אוֹ עַל פִּי עַצְמוֹ.** אֲפִלּוּ אֵין עֵדִים שֶׁנִּסְתְּרָה אֶלָּא עֵד אֶחָד, אוֹ הוּא עַצְמוֹ אוֹמֵר רְאִיתִיהָ שֶׁנִּסְתְּרָה אַחַר שֶׁקִּנֵּאתִי לָהּ, נֶאֶסְרָה בִּסְתִירָה זוֹ עַד שֶׁתִּשְׁתֶּה, שֶׁסְּתִירָתָהּ אוֹסַרְתָּהּ עָלָיו מִסָּפֵק:


על פי עד אחד או ע"פ עצמו – even if there aren’t other than one witness that she secluded herself, or he himself states: “I saw her that she secluded herself [with him] after I suspected her of being unfaithful, she is forbidden through this woman’s retirement with a man under suspicious circumstances, until she drinks [the bitter waters], for her suspected retirement with this man under suspicious circumstances prohibits her to him (i.e., her husband) from a doubt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:1:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** צָרִיךְ שְׁנֵי עֵדִים אַף לִסְתִירָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ. וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן אָמְרִינַן בַּגְּמָרָא דְּבַזְּמַן הַזֶּה לֹא לֵימָא אֱינִישׁ לְבֵיתֵיהּ אֲפִלּוּ בֵּינוֹ לְבֵינָהּ אַל תִּסָּתְרִי עִם אִישׁ פְּלוֹנִי, שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָחוּשׁ לְדִבְרֵי רַבִּי יוֹסֵי בְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה דְּאָמַר מְקַנֵּא אָדָם לְאִשְׁתּוֹ עַל פִּי עֵד אֶחָד אוֹ עַל פִּי עַצְמוֹ, וְאִם נִסְתְּרָה אַחַר שֶׁקִּנֵּא לָהּ בֵּינוֹ לְבֵינָהּ, נֶאֶסְרָה עָלָיו אִסּוּר עוֹלָם בַּזְּמַן הַזֶּה שֶׁאֵין שָׁם מֵי הַמָּרִים:


ר' יהושע אומר כו' – one needs two witnesses even for the woman’s retirement with a man under suspicious circumstances. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua and even though we state in the Gemara (Talmud Sotah 2b) that at this time, a man should not say to his wife, even between him an her [alone] that she should not seclude herself with that particular man, for one fears for the words of Rabbi Yose the son of Rabbi Yehuda who stated that a warning [is effective] if given by one witness or on [the husband’s personal testimony. If she then secluded herself with the man, since we do no have now he water for a suspected woman to test her, the husband forbids her to himself for all time.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:2:1)


**כֵּיצַד הוּא מְקַנֵּא לָהּ אָמַר לָהּ בִּפְנֵי שְׁנַיִם אַל תְּדַבְּרִי עִם אִישׁ פְּלוֹנִי כוּ'.** בַּגְּמָרָא מְפָרֵשׁ לְמַתְנִיתִין הָכִי, אָמַר לָהּ בִּפְנֵי שְׁנַיִם אַל תְּדַבְּרִי וְדִבְּרָה, אַל תְּדַבְּרִי וְנִסְתְּרָה, וְלֹא כְּלוּם הוּא וְאֵינָהּ אֲסוּרָה לֹא לְבַעְלָהּ וְלֹא לַתְּרוּמָה. אָמַר לָהּ אַל תִּסָּתְרִי וְדִבְּרָה עִמּוֹ שֶׁלֹּא בַּסֵּתֶר, עֲדַיִן מֻתֶּרֶת לְבַעְלָהּ וּמֻתֶּרֶת לֶאֱכֹל בַּתְּרוּמָה אִם אֵשֶׁת כֹּהֵן הִיא: נִכְנְסָה עִמּוֹ לְבֵית הַסֵּתֶר וְשָׁהֲתָה עִמּוֹ כְדֵי טֻמְאָה אֲסוּרָה לְבֵיתָהּ וַאֲסוּרָה לֶאֱכֹל בַּתְּרוּמָה. וְשִׁעוּר כְּדֵי טֻמְאָה, כְּדֵי לִצְלוֹת בֵּיצַת תַּרְנְגֹלֶת וּלְגָמְעָהּ:


כיצד הוא מקנא לה אמר לה בפני שנים אל תדברי עם איש פלוני כו' – In the Gemara (Talmud Sotah 5b): He said in the presence of two [witnesses], “do not speak [with him]” and she speaks [with him], or “do not speak with him” and she secludes herself [with him], and it is nothing, and she is not prohibited neither to husband nor to [partaking of] Priest’s due–Terumah (if her husband is a Kohen). If he said to her, “do not seclude yourself [with him] and she spoke with him, for she was not yet secluded, she is permitted to her husband and she is permitted to eat Terumah if she is the wife of a Kohen. If she went with him into the house of seclusion and stayed with him [long enough] that she is impure, she is forbidden to her husband and forbidden to consume Terumah. And the measurement [of time] to become ritually impure is [the time it takes] to roast the egg of a chicken and to quaff it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:2:2)


**וְאִם מֵת חוֹלֶצֶת וְלֹא מִתְיַבָּמֶת.** וְאִם מֵת בַּעְלָהּ קֹדֶם שֶׁיַּשְׁקֶנָּה, חוֹלֶצֶת וְלֹא מִתְיַבֶּמֶת. דִּכְתִיב בְּאִשָּׁה שֶׁמָּצָא בָּהּ עֶרְוַת דָּבָר (דברים כד) וְיָצְאָה מִבֵּיתוֹ וְהָלְכָה וְהָיְתָה לְאִישׁ אַחֵר, וְדָרְשִׁינַן לְאִישׁ אַחֵר וְלֹא לַיָּבָם. וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן אֵינָהּ מֻתֶּרֶת לְזָר בְּלֹא חֲלִיצָה, שֶׁכְּשֵׁם שֶׁאִם הָיָה בַּעְלָהּ חַי לֹא הָיְתָה מֻתֶּרֶת בְּלֹא גֵּט, כָּךְ אֵינָהּ מֻתֶּרֶת מִן הַיָּבָם בְּלֹא חֲלִיצָה:


אם מת חולצת ולא מתיבמת – but if her husband dies prior to causing her to drink [the bitter waters], she is released by Halitzah (i.e., she removes the shoe of her dead husband’s brother [and spits in his face]) and does not engage in a levirate marriage [with her dead husband’s brother], as it is written (Deuteronomy 24:2): “She leaves his household and becomes the wife of another man.” And they (i.e., the Rabbis) expound: “to another man” but not to the levir, and even though she is not permitted to a stranger without Halitzah, for just as if her husband were alive, she would not be permitted [to another man] without a Get–Jewish bill of divorce, so similarly, she is not permitted from the levir without Halizah.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:3:1)


**וְאֵלּוּ אֲסוּרוֹת לֶאֱכֹל בַּתְּרוּמָה.** לְעוֹלָם. וַאֲפִלּוּ הִיא בַּת כֹּהֵן. וְאִלּוּ שְׁאָר סוֹטוֹת אֵינָן אֲסוּרוֹת אֶלָּא עַד שֶׁיִּשְׁתּוּ וְיִמָּצְאוּ טְהוֹרוֹת:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:3:2)


**וְשֶׁבָּאוּ עֵדִים שֶׁהִיא טְמֵאָה.** וַאֲפִלּוּ לְאַחַר שֶׁשָּׁתְתָה וְלֹא בָּדְקוּהָ הַמַּיִם, אֲסוּרָה אִסּוּר עוֹלָם. לְפִי שֶׁאֵין הַמַּיִם בּוֹדְקִים בְּאִשָּׁה שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ עֵדִים שֶׁנִּטְמֵאת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (במדבר ה) וְנִסְתְּרָה וְהִיא נִטְמָאָה וְעֵד אֵין בָּהּ, דְּלֵיכָּא דְּיָדַע בָּהּ הוּא שֶׁהַמַּיִם בּוֹדְקִים, אֲבָל אִיכָּא דְּיָדַע בָּהּ, אֲפִלּוּ הָעֵדִים הֵם בִּמְדִינַת הַיָּם, אֵין הַמַּיִם בּוֹדְקִים:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:3:3)


**וְשֶׁבַּעְלָהּ בָּא עָלֶיהָ בַדֶּרֶךְ.** דְּשׁוּב אֵין הַמַּיִם בּוֹדְקִים אוֹתָהּ, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר ה) וְנִקָּה הָאִישׁ מֵעָוֹן, בִּזְמַן שֶׁהָאִישׁ מְנֻקֶּה מֵעָוֹן, שֶׁלֹּא בָּא עָלֶיהָ לְאַחַר סְתִירָה, אָז וְהָאִשָּׁה הַהִיא תִּשָּׂא אֶת עֲוֹנָהּ. אֵין הָאִישׁ מְנֻקֶּה מֵעָוֹן, אֵין הַמַּיִם בּוֹדְקִים אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:3:4)


**שְׁנֵי תַלְמִידֵי חֲכָמִים.** שֶׁיֵּדְעוּ לְהַתְרוֹת בּוֹ:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:3:5)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר בַּעְלָהּ נֶאֱמָן עָלֶיהָ.** אִם הַבַּעַל נֶאֱמָן עַל אִשְׁתּוֹ בְּאִסּוּר חָמוּר שֶׁל נִדָּה, לֹא יְהֵא נֶאֱמָן בְּאִסּוּר קַל שֶׁל לָאו גְּרֵידָא. וְטַעֲמַיְהוּ דְּרַבָּנָן, מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁאִסּוּר קַל הוּא, תַּקִּיף עֲלֵיהּ יִצְרֵיהּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:4:1)


**הָיוּ מַעֲלִין אוֹתָהּ לְבֵית דִּין הַגָּדוֹל.** דִּכְתִיב בְּסוֹטָה (שם) וְעָשָׂה לָהּ הַכֹּהֵן אֶת כָּל הַתּוֹרָה הַזֹּאת, וּכְתִיב הָתָם (דברים יז) עַל פִּי הַתּוֹרָה אֲשֶׁר יוֹרוּךָ, מַה לְּהַלָּן בְּבֵית דִּין הַגָּדוֹל אַף כָּאן בְּבֵית דִּין הַגָּדוֹל:


היו מעלים אותה לב"ד הגדול – as it is written concerning the woman suspected by her husband of having been unfaithful (Numbers 5:30): “And the priest shall carry out all of this ritual (i.e., Torah) with her,” and it is written there (Deuteronomy 17:11): “You shall act in accordance with the instructions (i.e., Torah) given you.” Just as there it takes place in the High Jewish Court, so here too, in the High Jewish Court.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:4:2)


**וּמְאַיְּמִין עָלֶיהָ.** כְּדֵי שֶׁתּוֹדֶה:


ומאיימין עליה – in order that she will confess.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:4:3)


**הַרְבֵּה יַלְדוּת עוֹשָׂה.** וְגוֹרֵם לְיַלְדָּה שֶׁתָּבֹא לִידֵי קִלְקוּל:


הרבה ילדות עושה – and causes to give birth to moral corruption.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:4:4)


**דְּבָרִים שֶׁאֵינָן כְּדַאי לְשׁוֹמְעָן.** שֶׁאֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה לִשְׁמֹעַ אוֹתָן הַדְּבָרִים שֶׁאוֹמְרִים לְפָנֶיהָ מִמַּעֲשֵׂיהֶן שֶׁל צַדִּיקִים, כְּגוֹן יְהוּדָה הוֹדָה וְלֹא בּוֹשׁ בְּמַעֲשֵׂה תָּמָר, רְאוּבֵן הוֹדָה וְלֹא בּוֹשׁ בְּמַעֲשֵׂה בִּלְהָה:


דברים שאינן כדאי לשומען – which is not appropriate to hear these things that they say in her presence from the deeds of the righteous people such as Judah who admitted and was not ashamed for the incident with Tamar. Reuven admitted and was not ashamed with the episode of Bilhah.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:1)


**שׁוֹבֶרֶת כְּתֻבָּתָהּ.** כּוֹתֶבֶת שׁוֹבֵר עַל כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, זָנִיתִי וְאִבַּדְתִּי כְּתֻבָּתִי, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תוּכַל לַחְזֹר וְלִתְבֹּעַ כְּתֻבָּתָהּ מִמֶּנּוּ. וּבְמָקוֹם שֶׁאֵין כּוֹתְבִין כְּתֻבָּה אֶלָּא סוֹמְכִין עַל תְּנַאי בֵּית דִּין הוּא שֶׁכּוֹתֶבֶת שׁוֹבֵר, אֲבָל בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁכּוֹתְבִים כְּתֻבָּה, מְקָרַעַת כְּתֻבָּתָהּ וְאֵינָהּ כּוֹתֶבֶת שׁוֹבֵר:


שוברת כתובתה – she writes a receipt on her Ketubah–marriage contract: “I have run about as a prostitute–been unfaithful and I have lost [the right to] my Ketubah in order that she would not be able to return and claim her Ketubah from him, but in a place where they do not write a Ketubah, but rather rely upon a condition of the Jewish court, is where she writes a receipt, but in a place where they write a Ketubah, she tears up her Ketubah and does not write a receipt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:2)


**מַעֲלִין אוֹתָהּ לְשַׁעַר הַמִּזְרָח.** מַעֲלִין וּמוֹרִידִין אוֹתָהּ כְּדֵי לְיַגְּעָהּ, אוּלַי תִטָּרֵף דַּעְתָּהּ וְתוֹדֶה:


מעלין אותה לשער המזרח – they bring her up and take her down in order to tire her so that perhaps her mind will become insane and she will confess.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:3)


**שֶׁעַל פֶּתַח שַׁעַר נִקָּנוֹר.** אָדָם שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ נִקָּנוֹר שֶׁהֵבִיא דְּלָתוֹת לְאוֹתוֹ פֶּתַח מֵאֶלֶכְּסַנְדְרִיָּא שֶׁל מִצְרַיִם וְנַעֲשׂוּ לוֹ נִסִּים וְנִקְרָא שַׁעַר נִקָּנוֹר עַל שְׁמוֹ כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בְּיוֹמָא [דַּף לח]:


שעל פתח שער נקנור – a man whose name is Nicanor who brought doors to that opening from Alexandria of Egypt and miracles occurred for him and it is called the Gate of Nicanor in his name as we state in [Tractate] Yoma [38a].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:4)


**שֶׁשָּׁם מַשְׁקִין אֶת הַסּוֹטוֹת.** דְּבָעִינַן לִפְנֵי ה':


ששם משקין את הסוטות – since we require “before God.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:5)


**וּמְטַהֲרִין אֶת הַיּוֹלְדוֹת.** כְּדֵי שֶׁיַּעַמְדוּ עַל גַּבֵּי קָרְבָּנָן. וְהוּא הַדִּין זָבִין וְזָבוֹת הַטְּעוּנִים קָרְבָּן:


ומטהרין את היולדות – in order that they will stand on top of their sacrifices and the same law applies regarding men and women with a flux which require a sacrifice.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:6)


**וּמְטַהֲרִין אֶת הַמְּצֹרָעִים.** כְּדִכְתִיב (ויקרא יד) וְהֶעֱמִיד הַכֹּהֵן הַמְטַהֵר וְכוּ'‏ לִפְנֵי ה':


ומטהרין את המצורע – as it is written (Leviticus 14:11): “These shall be presented before the LORD [with the man to be cleansed at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting] by the priest who performs the cleansing.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:7)


**אוֹחֵז בִּבְגָדֶיהָ.** בְּבֵית הַצַּוָּאר שֶׁלָּהֶן:


אוחז בבגדיה – at their front of the neck.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:8)


**אִם נִקְרְעוּ.** אֵינוֹ חוֹשֵׁשׁ, וְאִם נִפְרְמוּ אֵינוֹ חוֹשֵׁשׁ. פְּרִימָה גְּדוֹלָה מִקְּרִיעָה, שֶׁנִּקְרַע לִקְרָעִים הַרְבֵּה. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר, פְּרִימָה מִן הַצְּדָדִין קְרִיעָה בָּאֹרֶךְ:


אם נקרעו – he does not suspect and if they were torn, he does not suspect for the rending of garments is greater than rending, which was rent into many torn pieces. Another language: tearing from the sides is tearing by length.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:9)


**עַד שֶׁמְּגַלֶּה אֶת לִבָּהּ.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר ה) וּפָרַע אֶת רֹאשׁ הָאִשָּׁה, אֵין לִי אֶלָּא רֹאשָׁהּ, גּוּפָה מִנַּיִן, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר הָאִשָּׁה, אִם כֵּן מַה תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר אֶת רֹאשׁ, מְלַמֵּד שֶׁסּוֹתֵר אֶת שְׂעָרָהּ:


עד שמגלה את לבה – as it is written (Numbers 5:18): “[the priest] shall bare the woman’s head.” I don’t know anything other than her head; her body – from where? The inference teaches us: “the woman.” If so, what is the inference concerning “her head?” It teaches that he cuts her hair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:5:10)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** שֶׁמָּא תֵּצֵא זַכָּאָה וְיִתְגָּרוּ בָּהּ פִּרְחֵי כְהֻנָּה שֶּׁרָאוּ לִבָּהּ נָאֶה וְשַׂעֲרָהּ נָאֶה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה אומר כו' – perhaps she goes out innocent and the young priests become jealous of her for they saw that her heart was pleasant and her beautiful hair, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:6:1)


**הָיְתָה מִתְכַּסָּה בִלְבָנִים מְכַסָּהּ בִשְׁחוֹרִים.** וְאִם הָיוּ שְׁחוֹרִים יָפִין לָהּ, מְכַסָּהּ בִּבְגָדִים מְכֹעָרִים:


היתה מכוסה בלבנים מכסה בשחורים – if they were black [clothing] that were nice for her, cover her with ugly black clothing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:6:2)


**קַטְלָיוֹת.** עֲנָקִים שֶׁמְּשִׂימִים סְבִיב הַצַּוָּאר. וּמִפְּנֵי שֶׁהָאִשָּׁה חוֹנֶקֶת עַצְמָהּ בָּהֶם כְּדֵי שֶׁתֵּרָאֶה בַּעֲלַת בָּשָׂר נִקְרָאִים קַטְלָיוֹת:


קטליות – large that they place around the neck and because the woman chokes herself with them in order that she would appear to have a lot of skin, they are called קטליות –chains–necklaces.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:6:3)


**נְזָמִים וְטַבָּעוֹת מַעֲבִירִים מִמֶּנָּה.** מְפָרֵשׁ בַּגְּמָרָא דְּלֹא תֵימָא שֶׁיָּנִיחוּ לָהּ הַנְּזָמִים וְהַטַּבָּעוֹת אַחַר שֶׁקָּרְעוּ בְּגָדֶיהָ וְגִלּוּ אֶת לִבָּהּ, שֶׁזֶּה יוֹתֵר גְּנַאי לָהּ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁגְּנַאי יוֹתֵר לְאָדָם עָרוּם שֶׁיֵּלֵךְ בְּמִנְעָלִים בְּרַגְלָיו מִלֵּילֵךְ יָחֵף, קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּלֹא:


נזמים וטבעות מעבירים ממנה – The Gemara (Talmud Sotah 8b) explains that you would not say that she should leave the earrings and rings after her clothes had been torn and revealed her heart–bosom that this is more of a disgrace to her just as it is a greater disgrace for a naked man that he should walk with shoes on his feet than walk barefoot. It comes to teach us that this is not the case.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:6:4)


**חֶבֶל מִצְרִי.** מְפָרֵשׁ בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי, לְפִי שֶׁעָשְׂתָה מַעֲשֵׂה מִצְרַיִם. וְאִם אֵין חֶבֶל מִצְרִי מֵבִיא שְׁאָר חֲבָלִים:


חבל מצרי – It explains in the Jerusalem Talmud that because she acted in the ways of Egypt, but if there is no “common rope”–Egyptian rope, bring other ropes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:6:5)


**וְקוֹשְׁרוֹ לְמַעְלָה מִדַּדֶּיהָ.** שֶׁלֹּא יִפְּלוּ בְּגָדֶיהָ וְתִשָּׁאֵר עֲרוּמָה:


קושרו למעלה מדדיה – so that her clothing doesn’t fall and she would remain naked.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:6:6)


**וְכָל הָרוֹצֶה לִרְאוֹת בָּא לִרְאוֹת.** וְדַוְקָא בַּאֲנָשִׁים רְשׁוּת וְאֵינוֹ חוֹבָה. אֲבָל בְּנָשִׁים, חוֹבָה עַל כָּל הַנָּשִׁים הַמְּצוּיוֹת שָׁם לָבֹא וְלִרְאוֹת, כְּדִכְתִיב וְנִוַּסְּרוּ כָל הַנָּשִׁים:


וכל הרוצה לראות יבא ויראה – and specifically, with men, it is optional and not obligatory, but with women, it is obligatory upon all the women that are found there to come and look as it is written (Ezekiel 23:48): “and all the women shall take warning [not to imitate your wantonness].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:6:7)


**וְכָל הַנָּשִׁים מֻתָּרוֹת לִרְאוֹתָהּ.** חַיָּבִין לִרְאוֹתָהּ. הָכִי מְפָרֵשׁ לַהּ בַּגְּמָרָא:


וכל הנשים מותרות לראותה. חייבין לראות – But women are permitted to look at her and obligated to look at her. This is explained in the Gemara (Talmud Sotah 8b).



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:7:1)


**הַמָּקוֹם נִוְּלָהּ.** שֶׁמְּגַלֶּה כֹּהֵן אֶת רֹאשָׁהּ וְסוֹתֵר אֶת שַׂעֲרָה וְקוֹרֵעַ אֶת בְּגָדֶיהָ:


המקום ניולה – that a Kohen reveals her head and unravels her hair and rends her clothing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:7:2)


**הִיא גִלְּתָה אֶת עַצְמָהּ.** יָשְׁבָה בְּפָרָשַׁת דְּרָכִים וְגִלְּתָה עַצְמָהּ לַנּוֹאֵף:


היא גלתה את עצמה – she sat at the crossroads and revealed herself to an adulterer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:7:3)


**הַמָּקוֹם גִּלָּה עָלֶיהָ.** שֶׁכֹּהֵן מַעֲמִידָהּ עַל שַׁעַר נִקָּנוֹר וּמַרְאֶה קְלוֹנָהּ לַכֹּל:


המקום גלה עליה – that a Kohen places her at the Nicanor Gate and shows her disgrace to everyone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:7:4)


**בַּיָּרֵךְ הִתְחִילָה בָעֲבֵרָה תְחִלָּה.** בְּדֶרֶךְ תַּשְׁמִישׁ הַיָּרֵךְ נֶהֱנֶה תְּחִלָּה בְּקֵרוּב בָּשָׂר:


בירך התחילה בעבירה תחילה – on the way to sexual intercourse, the thigh benefits first through the closeness of the flesh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:7:5)


**וּשְׁאָר כָּל הַגּוּף לֹא פָלֵט.** שְׁאָר אֶבְרֵי גּוּפָהּ אֵינָם פְּלֵטִים מִן הַחֹלִי, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא הֻזְכְּרוּ בַּמִּקְרָא אֶלָּא בֶּטֶן וְיָרֵךְ:


ושאר כל הגוף לא פלט the other limbs of her body are not spared from the illness, even though they are not mentioned in the Bible other than the stomach and the thigh.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:8:1)


**לוֹנְבִיּוֹת.** חֲנִיתוֹת:


לונביאות – spears.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:8:2)


**לֵב אָבִיו.** אָמַר לוֹ אַבְשָׁלוֹם לְאָבִיו, כְּתֹב לִי שֶׁיֵּלְכוּ עִמִּי שְׁנַיִם אִישׁ שֶׁאֶבְחַר מִכָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל, כָּתַב לוֹ. וְהָיָה מַרְאֶה חוֹתָמוֹ שֶׁל אָבִיו לִשְׁנַיִם, וְהוֹלְכִים אַחֲרָיו, לִשְׁנַיִם אֲחֵרִים וְהוֹלְכִים אַחֲרָיו, עַד שֶׁכִּנֵּס מָאתַיִם אִישׁ, כֻּלָּן רָאשֵׁי סַנְהֶדְרָאוֹת. הֲרֵי שֶׁגָּנַּב לֵב אָבִיו וְלֵב בֵּית דִּין. וְלֵב כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל, דִּכְתִיב וַיְּגַנֵּב אַבְשָׁלוֹם אֶת לֵב אַנְשֵׁי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


לב אביו – Absalom said to his father (i.e., King David): “Write for me that two people that I will choose from all of Israel will go with me.” He wrote for him. He would show his father’s signature to two [people] and they follow him. [He then shows it to] two others and they follow him until he gathered two hundred men. All of them are heads of the Sanhedrins. For he stole the heart of his father, the heart of the Jewish court and the heart of all Israel, as it is written (II Samuel 15:6): “Thus Absalom won away the hearts of the men of Israel.”



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/1:9:1)


**וְכֵן לְעִנְיַן הַטּוֹבָה.** נוֹתְנִים לוֹ לָאָדָם שָׂכָר מֵעֵין הַטּוֹבָה שֶׁעָשָׂה. וּמִיהוּ יוֹתֵר מִמַּה שֶּׁעָשָׂה מִשְׁתַּלֵּם, דְּמִדָּה טוֹבָה מְרֻבָּה בְּתַשְׁלוּמִין מִמִּדַּת פֻּרְעָנוּת:


וכן לענין הטובה – we give a man a reward of the nature of the good that he did. But, however, more than what he did he is recompensed, for a good measure is greater in payments than the measure of retribution.



## Chapter 2


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:1)


**הָיָה מֵבִיא.** הַבַּעַל:


היה מביא – the husband.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:2)


**אֶת מִנְחָתָהּ.** כְּדִכְתִיב (במדבר ה) וְהֵבִיא אֶת קָרְבָּנָהּ:


את מנחתה – as it is written (Numbers 5:15): “[The man shall bring his wife to the priest.] And he shall bring as an offering for her [one-tenth of an ephah of barley flour].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:3)


**בִּכְפִיפָה מִצְרִית.** סַל עָשׂוּי מִצּוּרֵי הַדֶּקֶל מִן (הרך) [זְרָדִים] הַגְּדֵלִים סְבִיבוֹת הַדֶּקֶל:


בכפיפה מצרית – a basket made from palm bark [from] the soft that grew around the palm tree.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:4)


**כְּדֵי לְיַגְּעָהּ.** שֶׁתּוֹדֶה וְלֹא יִמָּחֶה הַשֵּׁם עַל הַמַּיִם. וּלְמַאן דְּאָמַר שֶׁבִּתְחִלָּה הָיָה מַשְׁקֶה אוֹתָהּ וְאַחַר כָּךְ מֵבִיא מִנְחָתָהּ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּמְחַק הַשֵּׁם, מְיַגְּעָהּ כְּדֵי שֶׁתּוֹדֶה וְלֹא תָמוּת מִיתָה מְנֻוֶּלֶת, שֶׁאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּמְחֲקָה הַמְּגִלָּה אִם אָמְרָה טְמֵאָה אֲנִי אֵינָהּ שׁוֹתָה:


כדי ליגעה – that she should confess [her sin of adultery] and not blot out the Name [of God] on the [bitter] waters. And according to the one who says that at the beginning, they would cause her to drink [the bitter waters], and afterwards bring her meal offering, even though the Name [of God] was blotted out, from when she hired, in order that she would confess and not die a disgraced–disfigured death, and even though the scroll was blotted out, if she said, “I am ritually impure,” she does not drink [the waters of bitterness].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:5)


**תְּחִלָּתָן וְסוֹפָן בִּכְלִי שָׁרֵת.** לָאו בִּכְלִי שָׁרֵת מַמָּשׁ קָאָמַר, שֶׁאֵין אָדָם מֵבִיא מִנְחָתוֹ מִתּוֹךְ בֵּיתוֹ בִּכְלִי שָׁרֵת, אֶלָּא בִּקְלָתוֹת שֶׁל כֶּסֶף וְשֶׁל זָהָב שֶׁהֵם רְאוּיִים לִכְלֵי שָׁרֵת:


תחלתן וסופן בכלי שרת – not in actual vessels dedicated to Temple service–sacred vessels is spoken of for a person does not bring his meal-offering from the midst of his home in sacred vessels but rather in women’s work baskets of silver or of gold which are appropriate for use as sacred vessels.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:6)


**וְזוֹ בִּכְפִיפָה מִצְרִית.** שֶׁאֵין רָאוּי לַעֲשׂוֹת מִמֶּנָּה כְּלִי שָׁרֵת, דְּדָמִי לְהַקְרִיבֵהוּ נָא לְפֶחָתֶךָ (מלאכי א):


וזו בכפיפה מצרית – And this is in a basket made of palm-twigs for it is not appropriate to make from it sacred vessels for it is similar to (Malachi 1:8): “Just offer it to your governor.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:7)


**כָּל הַמְּנָחוֹת טְעוּנוֹת שֶׁמֶן וּלְבֹנָה.** בַּגְּמָרָא פָּרֵיךְ, וְהָא אִיכָּא מִנְחַת חוֹטֵא דִּכְתִיב בָּהּ (ויקרא ה) לֹא יָשִׂים עָלֶיהָ שֶׁמֶן וְלֹא יִתֵּן עָלֶיהָ לְבֹנָה. וְכֵן הָא דִּתְנִינַן כָּל הַמְּנָחוֹת בָּאוֹת חִטִּים, קָשֶׁה, דְּהָא מִנְחַת הָעֹמֶר בָּאָה מִן הַשְּׂעֹרִים. וּמְתֹרָץ בַּגְּמָרָא לֹא תִתְנֵי מִלְּתָא בְּאַנְפֵּי נַפְשֵׁיהּ אֶלָּא כְּרֹךְ וְתָנֵי, וְהָכִי קָאָמַר, כָּל הַמְּנָחוֹת טְעוּנוֹת שֶׁמֶן וּלְבֹנָה, וְאוֹתָן שֶׁאֵינָם טְעוּנוֹת שֶׁמֶן וּלְבֹנָה בָּאוֹת מִן הַחִטִּין וּבָאוֹת סֹלֶת. מִנְחַת חוֹטֵא אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָהּ טְעוּנָה שֶׁמֶן וּלְבֹנָה בָּאָה חִטִּין וּבָאָה סֹלֶת. מִנְחַת הָעֹמֶר אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבָּאָה מִן הַשְּׂעֹרִין טְעוּנָה שֶׁמֶן וּלְבֹנָה:


כל המנחות טעונות שמן ולבונה – In the Gemara (Tractate Sotah 14b), the objection is raised: But this is the meal offering of sin-offering, as it is written about it (Leviticus 5:11): “he shall not add oil to it or lay frankincense on it, [for it is a sin offering].” But, that which we teach [in this Mishnah]: "כל המנחות באות חטים" –”All meal offerings derive from wheat,” is difficult, for this meal offering of the Omer comes from barley. And the Gemara (Talmud Sotah 14b) resolves the difficulty – one should not teach anything singly but rather, wrapped up–combined and it is taught and this is what it said: all of the meal offerings require oil and frankincense–"כל המנחות טעונות שמן ולבונה", but those that don’t require oil and frankincense come from wheat and the sin meal-offering comes from sifted fine flour, even though it does not require oil and frankincense, it comes from wheat and sifted fine flour.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:8)


**וּבָאָה גֶרֶשׂ.** וְזוֹ אֵינָהּ טְעוּנָה שֶׁמֶן וּלְבֹנָה וּבָאָה מִן הַשְּׂעֹרִים וּבָאָה קֶמַח:


And it comes from grits–unsifted flour, and this does not require oil or frankincense and comes from barley and is derived from flour.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:9)


**גֶּרֶשׂ.** מְבֹרָר וּמְנֻפֶּה בִּשְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה נָפָה כְּעֵין סֹלֶת בַּחִטִּים:


גרש – it is sifted and winnowed in thirteen sieves like a kind of fine sifted flour with wheat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:1:10)


**קֶמַח.** הַכֹּל מְעֹרָב כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּטְחַן:


קמח – everything is combined like it is grinded.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:2:1)


**הָיָה מֵבִיא.** הַכֹּהֵן:


היה מביא – the Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:2:2)


**פְּיָלִי.** כּוֹס בִּלְשׁוֹן יְוָנִי:


פיילי – a cup in the Greek language (see Talmud Sotah 15b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:2:3)


**מִן הַכִּיּוֹר.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר ה) מַיִם קְדוֹשִׁים, וְאֵין קְדוֹשִׁים אֶלָּא שֶׁנִּתְקַדְּשׁוּ בִּכְלִי:


מן הכיור – as it is written (Numbers 5:17): “[The priest shall take] sacral water [in an earthenware vessel],” but there is nothing sacred other than that which was sanctified in a utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:2:4)


**כְּשֵׁם שֶׁמְּמַעֵט.** רַבִּי יְהוּדָה בִּכְתַב הַמְּגִלָּה, לְקַמָּן בְּמַתְנִיתִין, כָּךְ מְמַעֵט כָּאן בַּמַּיִם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


כשם שממעט – Rabbi Yehuda, in the writing of the scroll (see Tractate Sotah, Chapter 2, Mishnah 3), further on in our Mishnah, so he lessens here [the amount of] the water, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:2:5)


**מָקוֹם הָיָה שָׁם.** נִכָּר מִתּוֹךְ שְׁאָר רִצְפַּת הַהֵיכָל:


מקום היה שם – amidst the rest of the floor of the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:2:6)


**וְטַבַּעַת קְבוּעָה בָהּ.** לְאוֹחֲזָהּ בְּטַבַּעְתָּהּ וּלְהַגְבִּיהָהּ מִשְּׁאָר הָרִצְפָּה שֶׁהִיא כֻּלָּהּ בְּאַבְנֵי שַׁיִשׁ:


וטבעת קבועה בה – to grab hold of the its ring and to lift it from the rest of the floor which was all of marble flagstone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:2:7)


**כְּדֵי שֶׁיֵּרָאֶה.** צָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּתֵּן בָּהּ שִׁעוּר שֶׁיְּהֵא נִרְאֶה וְנִכָּר עַל פְּנֵי הַמַּיִם:


כדי שיראה – he has to place in it a measure that will be seen and recognized on the face of the water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:2:8)


**וְנָתַן אֶל הַמַּיִם.** מִדְּלֹא כְּתִיב וְנָתַן בַּמַּיִם, אֶלָּא אֶל הַמַּיִם, מַשְׁמַע שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא נִבְלָע בְּתוֹכָן:


ונתן אל המים – since it is not written: “and place it in the water,” but rather (Numbers 5:17): “[the priest shall put it] into the water,” implying that it should not be absorbed into it.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:3:1)


**מֵאִם לֹא שָׁכַב אִישׁ אוֹתָךְ וְגוֹ'.** הִנָּקִי. שֶׁהֵן קְלָלוֹת הַבָּאוֹת מֵחֲמַת בְּרָכוֹת, דְּמַשְׁמַע הָא אִם שָׁכַב לֹא תִנָּקִי:


מאם לא שכב איש אותך וגו' – the clean–pure; which are the curses that come on account of blessings that imply that surely if a man laid, you would not be clean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:3:2)


**וְאֵינוֹ כּוֹתֵב וְהִשְׁבִּיעַ הַכֹּהֵן אֶת הָאִשָּׁה וְגוֹ'.** שֶׁאֵין זֶה אֶלָּא מִצְוָה עַל הַכֹּהֵן לְהַשְׁבִּיעָהּ:


ואינו כותב והשביע הכהן וגו' – for this is not other than a commandment upon the Kohen to have her take an oath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:3:3)


**וְלֹא הָיָה כּוֹתֵב וְאָמְרָה הָאִשָּׁה אָמֵן אָמֵן.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ אֶלָּא קַבָּלָה שֶׁהָאִשָּׁה מְקַבֶּלֶת עָלֶיהָ הָאָלוֹת. וְיָלֵיף לַהּ מִדִּכְתִיב הָאָלוֹת הָאֵלֶּה. הָאָלוֹת, לְרַבּוֹת קְלָלוֹת הַבָּאוֹת מֵחֲמַת בְּרָכוֹת. הָאֵלֶּה, לְמִעוּטֵי צַוָּאוֹת וְקַבָּלוֹת:


ולא היה כותב ואמרה האשה אמן אמן – it is nothing other than a receipt that the woman accepts upon herself the oaths and we derive it as it is written (Numbers 5:23): “[The priest shall put] these curses down in writing and rub it into the water of bitterness],” "האלות"–the curses – to include curses that come on account of the blessings. [The word] "האלה"–these – to exclude [the instructions] and the affirmations (see Talmud Sotah 17a – by the woman saying “Amen”).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:3:4)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר אֵינוֹ מַפְסִיק.** מִשֶּׁהִתְחִיל אִם לֹא שָׁכַב אִישׁ אוֹתָךְ עַד אָמֵן אָמֵן. וְכוֹתֵב צַוָּאוֹת דִּוְהִשְׁבִּיעַ הַכֹּהֵן אֶת הָאִשָּׁה וְקַבָּלוֹת דִּוְאָמְרָה הָאִשָּׁה אָמֵן אָמֵן, דְּדָרֵישׁ אֶת, לְרַבּוֹת צַוָּאוֹת וְקַבָּלוֹת:


ר' יוסי אומר אינו מפסיק – from when he starts: (Numbers 5:19): “If no man has lain with you” until (Numbers 5:24): “Amen, amen.” And he writes the instructions of (Numbers 5:21): “Here the priest shall administer the curse of adjuration to the woman” and the affirmations (Numbers 5:22): “And the woman shall say, ‘Amen, amen,’” for he expounds the word "את" (verse 19) to include instructions and affirmations.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:3:5)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר כָּל עַצְמוֹ אֵינוֹ כוֹתֵב.** כְּלוֹמַר הָיָה זָהִיר בְּעַצְמוֹ שֶׁלֹּא לִכְתֹּב אֶלָּא אָלוֹת בִּלְבַד, וְלֹא קְלָלוֹת הַבָּאוֹת מֵחֲמַת בְּרָכוֹת, וְלֹא צַוָּאוֹת, וְלֹא קַבָּלוֹת. דְּדָרֵישׁ הָאָלוֹת וְלֹא קְלָלוֹת הַבָּאוֹת מֵחֲמַת בְּרָכוֹת. הָאֵלֶּה, לְמִעוּטֵי צַוָּאוֹת וְקַבָּלוֹת:


רבי יהודה אומר כל עצמו אינו כותב – meaning to say that he was careful so as not to write other than "אלות"–oaths, and not curses that come on account of the blessings and not instructions nor affirmations, for he expounds [the word] "האלות"–the oaths – and not curses that come on account of these blessings to exclude instructions and affirmations.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:4:1)


**לוּחַ.** שֶׁל עֵץ:


לוח – of wood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:4:2)


**נְיָר.** שֶׁל עֲשָׂבִים שֶׁכּוֹתְשִׁים אוֹתָם וְעוֹשִׂים כְּמִין עוֹר:


נייר – of grasses–herbs that they pound–crush them and make them like a kind of leather.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:4:3)


**דִּפְתְּרָא.** עוֹר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְעֻבָּד כָּל צָרְכּוֹ, דִּמְלִיחַ וְקָמִיחַ וְלֹא עָפִיץ:


דפתרא – a hide which is not worked completely through which is prepared with salt and flour but not with gall-nut [juice].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:4:4)


**בַּסֵּפֶר.** בַּקְּלָף:


בספר – on parchment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:4:5)


**קוֹמוֹס.** שְׂרַף הָאִילָן, וּמַתִּיכוֹ בְמַיִם:


קומוס – resin of the tree which is melted in water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:4:6)


**קַנְקַנְתּוֹם.** אירמנ"ט. וּבְלַעַ"ז ודריאו"לו:


קנקנתום – IRMINT and in the foreign language “vitriol.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:4:7)


**וְלֹא בְכָל דָּבָר שֶׁהוּא רוֹשֵׁם.** וְנִבְלַע בַּקְּלָף וְאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לִמְחוֹת, דְּקוֹמוֹס וְקַנְקַנְתּוֹם עָבְדֵי רֹשֶׁם:


ולא בכל דבר שהוא רושם – and it is absorbed in the parchment and one cannot erase it for gum and vitriol make a lasting impression.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:5:1)


**אָמֵן שֶׁלֹּא שָׂטִיתִי אֲרוּסָה וּנְשׂוּאָה.** עַל יְדֵי גִּלְגּוּל שְׁבוּעָה הוּא מְגַלְגֵּל עָלֶיהָ זְנוּת שֶׁל אֵרוּסִין, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה בּוֹ בְּקִנּוּי וּסְתִירָה. וּמִכָּאן לְגִלְגּוּל שְׁבוּעָה מִן הַתּוֹרָה:


אמן שלא שטיתי ארוסה ונשואה – through the rule permitting the court to insert in an oath an affirmation to which the person concerned could not have been compelled directly, he places the burden upon her unchastity while betrothed and even though he did not have regarding him warnings of jealousy (given to the suspected wife) and a married woman’s retirement with a man under suspicious circumstances. And from here, this insertion of an oath to which a person could not have been compelled to take directly is from the Torah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:5:2)


**שׁוֹמֶרֶת יָבָם וּכְנוּסָה.** וְאִם הָיְתָה יְבִמְתּוֹ וּכְנָסָהּ, וְקִנֵּא לָהּ וְנִסְתְּרָה, מְגַלְגֵּל עָלֶיהָ טֻמְאָה שֶׁנִּטְמְאָה כְּשֶׁהִיא שׁוֹמֶרֶת יָבָם. וּמַתְנִיתִין רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא הִיא דְּאָמַר שׁוֹמֶרֶת יָבָם עֶרְוָה. וְאֵינָהּ הֲלָכָה, אֶלָּא שׁוֹמֶרֶת יָבָם שֶׁזִּנְּתָה מֻתֶּרֶת לַיָּבָם, לְפִיכָךְ אֵינוֹ מַתְנֶה עָלֶיהָ אִם זִנְּתָה שׁוֹמֶרֶת יָבָם:


שומרת יבם וכנוסה – if she was his sister-in-law (i.e., the widow of his brother who died without issue) and he brought her into a levirate marriage and he was jealous of her and she retired with a man under suspicious circumstances, he inserts a claim of ritual impurity which she could not have been compelled to take directly that she had become defiled while she was awaiting levirate marriage . And our Mishnah is according to Rabbi Akiva who said that a woman awaiting levirate marriage is unchastity–lewdness, but it is not the Halakha but rather a woman awaiting levirate marriage who ran about as a prostitute is permitted to the levir. Therefore, he does not make this condition upon her if she was unchaste as a woman awaiting levirate marriage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:5:3)


**אָמֵן שֶׁלֹּא נִטְמֵאתִי וְכוּ'.** פֵּרוּשֵׁי קָמְפָרֵשׁ לָאָמֵן עַל הָאָלָה אָמֵן עַל הַשְּׁבוּעָה דִּתְנִינַן לְעֵיל. וְהָכִי קָאָמַר, אָמֵן שֶׁלֹּא נִטְמֵאתִי הוּא אָמֵן עַל הַשְּׁבוּעָה שֶׁהִשְׁבִּיעָהּ הַכֹּהֵן מַשְׁבִּיעַ אֲנִי עָלַיִךְ שֶׁלֹּא נִטְמֵאת:


אמן שלא נטמאתי וכו' – My explanation is made for the “Amen” on the imprecation, “Amen” on the oath, as we taught above (in this Mishnah). And this is what it said: “Amen” that I did not become ritually impure – that is the “Amen” on the oath that the Kohen imposed upon her: “I impose upon you the oath that you did not become ritually impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:5:4)


**וְאִם נִטְמֵאתִי יָבֹאוּ בִי.** הַיְנוּ קַבָּלַת אָלָה, שֶׁאָמַר לָהּ הַכֹּהֵן וְאִם נִטְמֵאת יָבֹאוּ בָּךְ כָּל הַקְּלָלוֹת הַלָּלוּ:


ואם נטמאתי יבואו בי – and if I become I became defiled, it should come upon me,” meaning the acceptance of the imprecation that the Kohen related to her and if you were ritually defiled, all of these curses will come upon you.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:5:5)


**אָמֵן שֶׁלֹּא אֶטָּמֵא.** שֶׁאִם תִּטָּמֵא לְאַחַר זְמַן מַיִם מְעַרְעֲרִים אוֹתָהּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


אמן שלא אטמא – that if you become ritually impure afterwards, the water will contest her, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/2:6:1)


**וְלֹא מִשֶׁנִּתְגָּרְשָׁה.** שֶׁאִם זִנְּתָה לְאַחַר שֶׁגֵּרְשָׁה וְחָזַר וְהֶחְזִירָה וְקִנֵּא לָהּ וְנִסְתְּרָה, אֵין הַמַּיִם בּוֹדְקִים עַל זְנוּת שֶׁלְּאַחַר גֵּרוּשִׁין, דִּגְרוּשָׁה שֶׁזִּנְּתָה מֻתֶּרֶת לַחְזֹר לְבַעְלָהּ, דִּבְקִדּוּשִׁין תָּלָה רַחֲמָנָא הָאִסּוּר, לֹא בְבִיאָה, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כד) וְיָצְאָה מִבֵּיתוֹ וְהָלְכָה וְהָיְתָה לְאִישׁ אַחֵר. לְפִיכָךְ, נִתְקַדְּשָׁה לְאִישׁ אַחֵר, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נִבְעֲלָה, אֲסוּרָה לַחְזֹר לְבַעְלָהּ. וְאִם נִבְעֲלָה בְּלֹא קִדּוּשִׁין, מֻתֶּרֶת לַחְזֹר לְבַעְלָהּ:


ולא משנתגרשה – for if she ran about as a prostitute–was unchaste after she was divorced, and he retracted [from his decision to divorce her] and restored her [as his wife] and he was jealous of her, and she retired with a man under suspicious circumstances, the [bitter] waters do not examine her on prostitution that is after divorce, for the divorced woman who engaged in prostitution is permitted to return to her husband, for the All-Merciful (i.e., God) suspended [the prohibition] on Kiddushin–betrothal, [not] on intercourse, as it is written (Deuteronomy 24:2): “She leaves his household and becomes the wife of another man.” Therefore, when she was betrothe to another man, even though she had not had sexual intimacy, she is prohibited to return to her husband, but if she had sexual intercourse without Kiddushin–betrothal, she is permitted to return to her husband.



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:1:1)


**הָיָה נוֹטֵל.** בַּעְלָהּ אֶת הַמִּנְחָה, שֶׁאֵין מִצְוַת כְּהֻנָּה בִּמְנָחוֹת אֶלָּא מִקְּמִיצָה וָאֵילָךְ. וְהַגָּשָׁה נַמִּי תְּחִלַּת קְמִיצָה הִיא, שֶׁאֵין זָר קָרֵב לַמִּזְבֵּחַ. אֲבָל קִדּוּשֵׁי כְּלִי וִיצִיקָה וּבְלִילָה, בִּמְנָחוֹת הַטְּעוּנוֹת שֶׁמֶן, כָּשֵׁר בְּזָר:


היה נוטל – her husband [would take] her meal-offering, for the command of the priesthood regarding meal-offering is from taking a fistful of the meal-offering and onward (see also Talmud Menahot 9a) and bringing near [the meal-offering] is also the beginning of taking a fistful of the meal-offering but a foreigner (i.e., non-Kohen) does not make an offering on the altar but the sanctification of the utensils and the pouring of oil (on the flour – see Leviticus 2:1) and the mixing, as meal-offerings require oil are appropriate for non-Kohanim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:1:2)


**וְנוֹתְנָהּ עַל יָדָהּ.** לְהָנִיף:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:1:3)


**וְכֹהֵן מֵנִיחַ יָדוֹ תַּחְתֶּיהָ.** שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בִּשְׁלָמִים (ויקרא ז) יָדָיו תְּבִיאֶנָה, וְנֶאֱמַר בְּמִנְחַת סוֹטָה (במדבר ה) וְלָקַח הַכֹּהֵן מִיַּד הָאִשָּׁה, מַה בִּשְׁלָמִים כֹּהֵן מֵנִיחַ יָדוֹ תַּחַת יַד הַבְּעָלִים וּמֵנִיף, כָּךְ בְּמִנְחַת סוֹטָה כֹּהֵן מֵנִיחַ יָדוֹ תַּחַת יַד הָאִשָּׁה וּמֵנִיף:


וכהן מניח ידו תחתיה – as it states regarding peace offerings (Leviticus 7:30): “his own hands shall present [the LORD’s offerings by fire],” and it is stated regarding the meal-offering of the woman suspected of unfaithfulness by her husband (Numbers 5:25): “Then the priest shall take from the woman’s hand [the meal-offering of jealousy,” just as with peace-offerings, the Kohen places his hand underneath the hand of the owners and waves it, so also in the meal-offering of the woman suspected of unfaithfulness by her husband, the Kohen places his hand underneath the hand of the woman and waves it.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:2:1)


**וְהִגִּישׁ.** לְקֶרֶן מַעֲרָבִית דְּרוֹמִית:


והגיש – to the southwestern corner.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:2:2)


**וְהַשְּׁאָר נֶאֱכָל לַכֹּהֲנִים.** שֶׁכָּל הַנִּקְמָצוֹת שְׁיָרֵיהֶן נֶאֱכָלִים:


והשאר נאכל לכהנים – for all the grabbed [portions of the meal-offerings] are their remnants and are eaten.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:2:3)


**הָיָה מַשְׁקָהּ וְאַחַר כָּךְ מַקְרִיב אֶת מִנְחָתָהּ.** תְּלָתָא קְרָאֵי כְּתִיבֵי. וְהִשְׁקָהּ קַמָּא, וְאַחַר יַשְׁקֶה, וְהִשְׁקָהּ בַּתְרָא. וְהִשְׁקָהּ קַמָּא, לְגוּפֵיהּ, שֶׁמַּשְׁקֶה אַחַר שֶׁנִּמְחֲקָה הַמְּגִלָּה קֹדֶם הַקְטָרַת הַמִּנְחָה. וְאַחַר יַשְׁקֶה, אַף עַל גַּב דְּמַשְׁמַע דַּוְקָא, עַל כָּרְחָךְ מִקְרָא הָאָמוּר רִאשׁוֹן נִדְרָשׁ לְגוּפוֹ, וּמִקְרָאוֹת יְתֵרוֹת הָאֲמוּרוֹת אַחֲרָיו נִתְּנוּ לִדָּרֵשׁ, הִלְכָּךְ וְאַחַר יַשְׁקֶה אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּכְתַּב אַחַר הַקְטָרַת הַמִּנְחָה אַקֹּדֶם הַקְטָרָה קָאֵי, וְאַמְּחִיקָה הַכְּתוּבָה לְמַעְלָה קָאֵי, וְקָאָמַר דְּאִם לֹא נִמְחֲקָה יָפֶה וַעֲדַיִן רִשּׁוּמָן שֶׁל אוֹתִיּוֹת נִכָּר לֹא יַשְׁקֶנָּה. וְהִשְׁקָהּ בַּתְרָא, לְאַשְׁמוֹעִינַן שֶׁאִם נִמְחֲקָה הַמְּגִלָּה וְאָמְרָה אֵינִי שׁוֹתָה, מְעַרְעֲרִים אוֹתָהּ וּמַשְׁקִין אוֹתָהּ בְּעַל כָּרְחָהּ:


היה משקה ואחר כך מקריב את מנחתה – Three Biblical verses are written: The first [verse]: "והשקה"–and he is to make the woman drink [the waters of bitterness]” (Numbers 5:24) is first; and after that:"ישקה" –Last, he shall make [the woman] drink the water” (Numbers 5:26) and last "והשקה" –Once he made her drink [the water]” (Numbers 5:27). The first mention of "והשקה"–and he is to make [the woman] drink [the waters of bitterness” is for itself – that he causes her to drink after the scroll is blotted out before the offering of the meal-offering. And afterwards, "ישקה" –”and he [shall make the woman] drink the water” – even though that it implies that she was specifically forced, the Biblical verse that is mentioned first is expounded for itself. But the Biblical verses that are mentioned after this are given for being expounded. Therefore, and afterwards "ישקה"–”and he [shall make the woman] drink [the waters of bitterness],” even though it is written after the offering of the meal-offering it refers to preceding the offering. But, it refers to the blotting out [of the parchment] that is written above, as it states, that if it was not blotted out, it is well, but if the form of the letters are recognized, he should not cause her to drink [the waters of bitterness]. And the concluding "והשקה"–Once he made her drink [the water] comes to teach us that if the scroll was blotted out and she states, “I will not drink,” they chide–scold her and force her to drink [the waters of bitterness].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:2:4)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר מַקְרִיב אֶת מִנְחָתָהּ וְאַחַר כָּךְ מַשְׁקָהּ.** עַל כָּרְחֲךָ וְאַחַר יַשְׁקֶה דַּוְקָא הוּא וְדַוְקָא כָּתְבֵיהּ לְאַחַר הַקְטָרָה. וְהִשְׁקָהּ קַמָּא, שֶׁאִם בְּדִיעֲבַד הִשְׁקָהּ וְאַחַר כָּךְ הִקְרִיב מִנְחָתָהּ, כְּשֵׁרָה. וְהִשְׁקָהּ בַּתְרָא, שֶׁאִם נִמְחֲקָה הַמְּגִלָּה וְאָמְרָה אֵינִי שׁוֹתָה, מְעַרְעֲרִין אוֹתָהּ וּמַשְׁקִין אוֹתָהּ בְּעַל כָּרְחָהּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


רבי שמעון אומר מקריב את מנחתה ואחר כך ישקה – that [the words[ "ואחר ישקה"–”Last, he shall make [the woman] drink [the water]” – specifically him, and specifically he wrote it after the offering [of meal-offering], and the first mention of "והשקה" –”and he is to make the woman drink [the waters of bitterness],” that if he forced her to drink post-facto, and afterwards made the offering of her meal-offering, it is fit. And the last [mention]: "והשקה" –”Once he made her drink [the water], that if the scroll was blotted out and she stated, “I will not drink,” we chide–scold her and cause her to drink it against her will. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:3:1)


**מְגִלָּתָהּ נִגְנֶזֶת.** בְּצַד הַהֵיכָל:


מגילתה נגנזת – at the side of the Temple (in the hall containing the golden altar).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:3:2)


**וּמִנְחָתָהּ מִתְפַּזֶּרֶת עַל הַדָּשֶׁן.** וְנִשְׂרֶפֶת עַל בֵּית הַדֶּשֶׁן שֶׁהָיָה בָּעֲזָרָה שֶׁשּׂוֹרְפִים שָׁם פְּסוּלֵי קָדְשֵׁי קָדָשִׁים, דְּהוֹאִיל וְקָדְשָׁה בִּכְלִי שָׁרֵת טְעוּנָה שְׂרֵפָה בָּעֲזָרָה:


ומנחתה מתפזרת על הדשן – and is burned on the place where the ashes of the sacrifices were deposited that was in the Temple courtyard where they burn there the unfit things for the Holy of Holies, for since it was sanctified with the sacred vessels–dedicated for the Temple service, it requires burning in the courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:3:3)


**וְאֵין מְגִלָּתָהּ כְּשֵׁרָה לְהַשְׁקוֹת [בָּהּ] סוֹטָה אַחֶרֶת.** דְּבָעִינַן כְּתִיבָה לִשְׁמָהּ, דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְעָשָׂה לָהּ הַכֹּהֵן, כָּל עֲשִׂיָּתָהּ לִשְׁמָהּ:


ואין מגילתה כשרה לשקות [בה] סוטה אחרת – for we require writing for her sake, as it is written (there – Numbers 5:30): “and the priest shall carry out [all this ritual with her].” All of the carrying out of the ritual is for her sake.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:3:4)


**הַמַּיִם נִשְׁפָּכִין.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּאָמְרָה טְמֵאָה אֲנִי, בְּדוּקָה וְעוֹמֶדֶת הִיא. וּמַיִם הַמָּרִים לֹא נִתְּנוּ אֶלָּא לְבָרֵר בָּהֶם אֶת הַסָּפֵק:


המים נשפכים – for since she said hat “I am ritually impure,” she is examined and stands, and the waters of bitterness were not given [to her] other than to verify through them the doubt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:3:5)


**מְעַרְעֲרִים אוֹתָהּ.** דְּדִלְמָא טְהוֹרָה הִיא, וּמֵחֲמַת בְּעִיתוּתָא קָאָמְרָה:


מערערים אותה – for perhaps she is ritually pure, and on account of her being frightened she says it.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:1)


**אֵינָהּ מַסְפֶּקֶת לִשְׁתּוֹת וְכוּ'.** בַּגְּמָרָא מוֹכִיחַ דְּמַתְנִיתִין רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא הִיא דְּסָבַר כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דְּאָמַר מַקְרִיב מִנְחָתָהּ וְאַחַר כָּךְ מַשְׁקֶה אוֹתָהּ, דְּכַמָּה דְּלֹא קָרְבָה מִנְחָתָהּ לֹא בָּדְקֵי לָהּ מַיָּא, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר ה) מִנְחַת זִכָּרוֹן מַזְכֶּרֶת עָוֹן:


אינה מספקת לשתות – in the Gemara (Tractate Sotah 20b), that our Mishnah is according to Rabbi Akiva who holds like Rabbi Shimon who said that one offers her meal-offering and afterwards cause her to drink it. [for as long as] her meal-offering is not offered, the waters do not examine her, as it is written (Numbers 5:15): “a meal offering of remembrance which recalls wrongdoing.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:2)


**מִתְמַלֵּאת גִּידִין.** מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁבְּשָׂרָהּ נָפוּחַ נִרְאֵית כִּמְלֵאָה גִּידִים:


מתמלאת גידין – since her flesh blows up, she appears like her sinews have become swollen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:3)


**שֶׁלֹּא תְטַמֵּא אֶת הָעֲזָרָה.** לֹא מִטֻּמְאַת מֵת קָאָמַר, אֶלָּא שֶׁלֹּא תִפְרֹס נִדָּה וּתְטַמֵּא שַׁעַר נִקָּנוֹר וְעֶזְרַת נָשִׁים שֶׁתֵּצֵא דֶּרֶךְ שָׁם, שֶׁנִּדָּה וְזָבָה אֲסוּרִין לִכְנֹס בְּהַר הַבַּיִת. אֲבָל הַמֵּת עַצְמוֹ מֻתָּר לְהַכְנִיסוֹ לְהַר הַבַּיִת וּלְשַׁעַר נִקָּנוֹר, שֶׁלֹּא נִתְקַדְּשׁוּ בִּקְדֻשַּׁת הָעֲזָרָה:


שלא תטמא את העזרה – it is stated not from defilement from contact with the dead, but rather that she should not spread the condition of uncleanness from her period of menstruation and defile Nicanor [Gate] and the Women’s Court when she would go out through there for a menstruant woman and a woman with a flux are forbidden to enter the Temple mount. But a dead person himself is permitted to enter into the Temple Mount and [into] Nicanor [Gate] which were not sanctified with the holiness of the Temple Courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:4)


**יֶשׁ לָהּ זְכוּת וְכוּ'.** בַּגְּמָרָא מְפָרֵשׁ דִּזְכוּת דְּתוֹרָה הוּא דְּתוֹלֶה לָהּ, שֶׁהוֹלִיכָה בָּנֶיהָ לְבֵית הַסֵּפֶר וְהִמְתִּינָה לְבַעְלָהּ שֶׁיָּצָא חוּץ לָעִיר לַעֲסֹק בַּתּוֹרָה:


יש לה זכות וכו' – In the Gemara (Tractate Sotah 21a) it explains that the merit of [studying] the Torah is what suspends its effect for her as she brought her children to school and waited for her husband that he will leave outside of the city to engage in the study of Torah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:5)


**כְּאִלּוּ מְלַמְּדָהּ תִּפְלוּת.** חִבּוּר מִשְׁכַּב אֲנָשִׁים. שֶׁמִּתּוֹךְ הַתּוֹרָה הִיא מְבִינָה עַרְמִימוּת וְעוֹשָׂה דְּבָרֶיהָ בְּהֶצְנֵעַ. כְּדִכְתִיב (משלי ח) אֲנִי חָכְמָה שָׁכַנְתִּי עָרְמָה:


כאלו מלמדה תפלות – the juxtaposition of sexual connection of men that from the Torah she understands shrewdness and makes her words in chastity as it is written (Proverbs 8:12): “I, Wisdom, lives with prudence; [I attain knowledge and foresight]” (see Talmud Sotah 21b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:6)


**רוֹצָה אִשָּׁה בְקַב.** לִזּוֹן מְזוֹנוֹת מֻעָטִים, וִיהֵא תַּשְׁמִישׁ מָצוּי לָהּ:


רוצה אשה בקב – to feed a small amount of food so that sexual intercourse will be available to her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:7)


**מִתִּשְׁעָה קַבִּין וּפְרִישׁוּת.** לִפְרֹשׁ מִן הַתַּשְׁמִישׁ, הִלְכָּךְ אֵין טוֹב שֶׁתִּלְמֹד תּוֹרָה:


מתשעה קבין ופרישות – to separate from sexual intercourse; therefore, there is no good that is Torah study.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:8)


**חָסִיד שׁוֹטֶה.** מְפָרֵשׁ בַּגְּמָרָא כְּגוֹן דְּטָבְעָה אִשָּׁה בַּנָּהָר וְאָמַר לָאו אוֹרַח אַרְעָא לְאִסְתַּכּוּלֵי בְּאִתְּתָא וּלְאַצּוֹלַהּ:


חסיד שוטה – It explains in the Gemara (Talmud Sotah 21b) such as the example that a woman drowned in the river and he said: “It is not the way of the world to look at this woman and to save her.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:9)


**רָשָׁע עָרוּם.** כְּגוֹן הַמַּטְעִים דְּבָרָיו לַדַּיָּן קֹדֶם שֶׁיָּבֹא בַּעַל דִּין חֲבֵרוֹ. וּמִשֶּׁנִּפְתְּחוּ בְּלֵב הַדַּיָּן שַׁעֲרֵי זְכוּת לְדִבְרֵי זֶה, קָשֶׁה לְסַלְּקָן, וַהֲרֵי רִשְׁעָתוֹ וְעָרְמָתוֹ שֶׁל זֶה שֶׁעָבַר עַל לֹא תִשָּׂא שֵׁמַע שָׁוְא (שמות כג):


רשע ערום – such as the example where a person speaks in a misleading manner to the judge prior to his fellow litigant coming and when the gates of merit were opened in the heart of the judge to my words, it is difficult to remove them and behold, his wickedness and shrewdness of that [litigant] who transgressed on (Exodus 23:1): “You must not carry false rumors.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:10)


**וְאִשָּׁה פְרוּשָׁה.** כְּגוֹן אִשָּׁה כַּשְׁפָנִית שֶׁהָיְתָה עוֹצֶרֶת רֶחֶם הַנָּשִׁים הַיּוֹלְדוֹת בִּכְשָׁפֶיהָ, וּבְשָׁעָה שֶׁהָיְתָה הַיּוֹלֶדֶת מַקְשָׁה בְּלִדְתָּהּ הָיְתָה אוֹמֶרֶת אֵלֵךְ וְאֶתְפַּלֵּל אוּלַי תְּקֻבַּל תְּפִלָּתִי, וְסוֹתֶרֶת כְּשָׁפֶיהָ וְיוֹלֶדֶת:


ואשה פרושה – such as the example of a woman sorcerer who would close the womb of women that are giving birth through sorcery and the woman giving birth would have difficulty doing so. She would say: “I will go and pray; perhaps my prayer will be accepted and she dissolves her witchcraft and she gives birth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:11)


**וּמַכּוֹת פְּרוּשִׁים.** שֶׁמַּכֶּה עַצְמוֹ לְהַרְאוֹת שֶׁהוּא עָנָו וְצָנוּעַ, כְּגוֹן שֶׁמְּהַלֵּךְ עָקֵב בְּצַד גֻּדָּל וְאֵינוֹ מֵרִים רַגְלוֹ מִן הָאָרֶץ וּמִתּוֹךְ כָּךְ מְנַקֵּף אֶצְבְּעוֹתָיו בָּאֲבָנִים, וְהוּא שֶׁנִּקְרָא בַּגְּמָרָא פָּרוּשׁ נַקְפִּי. אוֹ מִי שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה עַצְמוֹ כְּעוֹצֵם עֵינָיו שֶׁלֹּא לְהִסְתַּכֵּל בַּנָּשִׁים, וּמִתּוֹךְ כָּךְ מַכֶּה רֹאשׁוֹ בַּכֹּתֶל וְיוֹצֵא מִמֶּנּוּ דָּם, וְהוּא הַנִּקְרָא פָּרוּשׁ קוֹזָאִי, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁמַּקִּיז דָּם מֵרֹאשׁוֹ מֵחֲמַת פְּרִישׁוּתוֹ:


ומכות פרושים –that he hits himself to show that he is humble and modest, like the example that he walks at the rear at the side of greatness and does not raise his foot from the ground and as a result knocks his fingers on stones and he is that which called in the Gemara (Talmud Sotah 22b) a knocker–borrower (an opprobrious epithet for a sort of sanctimonious Pharisees), or someone who makes himself as one who closes his eyes so as to not look at women. And because of this, he strikes his head and the wall and blood leaves him. And he who is called a bleeder, meaning to say that he bleeds himself as a result of his [excess] piety.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:4:12)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ מְכַלֵּי עוֹלָם.** שֶׁרוֹצִים לְהַרְאוֹת לַבְּרִיּוֹת שֶׁהֵם חֲסִידִים, וְאֵין תּוֹכָם כְּבָרָם:


הרי אלו מכלי עולם – that they want to demonstrate to humanity that they are pious but their inner being is not like their outer shell.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:5:1)


**מַדְהֶה אַתָּה אֶת הַמַּיִם.** מְחַסֵּר וּמַחְשִׁיךְ. הַגָּוֶן הֶחָסֵר נִקְרָא דִּהָה בִּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה:


מדחה אתה את הטוב – weakens and darkens. The missing resemblance is called “feeble”–”faint” in the language of the Mishnah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:5:2)


**מִתְנַוְּנָה וְהוֹלֶכֶת.** נֶחֱלֵית וְהוֹלֶכֶת:


מתנוונה והולכת – to continually become more faint.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:6:1)


**נִטְמֵאת מִנְחָתָהּ עַד שֶׁלֹּא קָדְשָׁה בַכְּלִי הֲרֵי הִיא כְּכָל הַמְּנָחוֹת.** שֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ עַד שֶׁלֹּא קִדְּשׁוּ בִכְלִי:


נטמאת מנחתה עד שלא קדשה בכלי. הרי היא ככל המנחות – that it was defiled before it was sanctified in a utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:6:2)


**וְתִפָּדֶה.** וְתֵצֵא לְחֻלִּין, וּמִן הַמָּעוֹת יִקְנֶה אַחֶרֶת:


ותפדה – and it is removed for non-holy produce, and from the monies [from the sale of it], he should purchase another.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:6:3)


**וְתִשָּׂרֵף.** שֶׁקָּדְשָׁה קְדֻשַּׁת הַגּוּף וְאֵינָהּ יוֹצְאָה עוֹד לְחֻלִּין:


ותשרף – when the body was sanctified with holiness and it doesn’t go out anymore for non-holy produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:6:4)


**וְכָל הַנְּשׂוּאוֹת לְכֹהֲנִים מִנְחוֹתֵיהֶן נִשְׂרָפוֹת.** וַאֲפִלּוּ קָרַב קֹמֶץ כְּהִלְכָתוֹ וְלֹא נִטְמֵאת, נִשְׂרָפִים שְׁיָרֶיהָ עַל בֵּית הַדֶּשֶׁן וְאֵינָן נֶאֱכָלִים, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לַכֹּהֵן בַּעְלָהּ חֵלֶק בָּהּ, שֶׁמִּשֶּׁלּוֹ הִיא בָּאָה, וְהַתּוֹרָה אָמְרָה (ויקרא ו) וְכָל מִנְחַת כֹּהֵן כָּלִיל תִּהְיֶה לֹא תֵאָכֵל, וְאֵין כֻּלָּהּ כָּלִיל מִפְּנֵי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ חֵלֶק בָּהּ שֶׁלְּכַפָּרָתָהּ הִיא בָּאָה, הָא כֵּיצַד, הַקֹּמֶץ קָרֵב בְּעַצְמוֹ וְהַשִּׁירַיִם קְרֵבִין בְּעַצְמָן:


וכל הנשואות לכהנים מנחותיהן נשרפות – and even if a fistful was brought according to the law and not defiled, its remnants are burned on the place where the ashes of sacrifices were deposited and are not consumed because her husband, the Kohen has a portion in it, for it comes from what is his , and the Torah stated (Leviticus 6:16): “So, too, every meal offering of a priest shall be a whole offering: it shall not be eaten.” But the bride is not a whole offering because she has a part in it, and it comes for her atonement. How is this so? The handful of the meal offering which the priest takes to be put on the altar is offered on its own and the left overs are offered on their own.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:7:1)


**וְכֹהֶנֶת שֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת לְיִשְׂרָאֵל.** בַּת כֹּהֵן:


כהנת שנשאת לישראל – the daughter of a Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:7:2)


**מִנְחָתָהּ נֶאֱכֶלֶת.** דְּכָל מִנְחַת כֹּהֵן כָּלִיל תִּהְיֶה כְּתִיב, כֹּהֵן וְלֹא כֹּהֶנֶת. וְכֵן כֹּהֶנֶת אַלְמָנָה וּגְרוּשָׁה שֶׁהֵבִיאָה מִנְחַת נְדָבָה, נֶאֱכֶלֶת:


מנחתה נאכלת – it is written (Leviticus 6:16): “So, too, every meal offering of a priest shall be a whole offering [it shall not be eaten].” A Kohen, but not the daughter of a Kohen, and similarly a daughter of a Priest who is a widow and–or a divorcee who brought a free-will donation of meal-offering is consumed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:7:3)


**כֹּהֶנֶת מִתְחַלֶּלֶת.** מִן הַתְּרוּמָה וּמִן הַכְּהֻנָּה, אִם נִבְעֲלָה לַפְּסוּלִים שׁוּב אֵינָהּ חוֹזֶרֶת לְכַשְׁרוּתָהּ לְעוֹלָם:


כהנת מתחללת – from the priest’s due and from the priesthood if she was engaged in a sexual affair to those who are unfit, she never can return to her state of fitness ever.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:7:4)


**כֹּהֵן אֵינוֹ מִתְחַלֵּל.** אִם נָשָׂא גְּרוּשָׁה חֲלָלָה אוֹ זוֹנָה, אֶלָּא כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהוּא עִמָּהּ הוּא פָּסוּל לָעֲבוֹדָה, מִשֶּׁגֵּרְשָׁהּ חוֹזֵר לְהֶכְשֵׁרוֹ, דִּכְתִיב (שם כא) וְלֹא יְחַלֵּל זַרְעוֹ, זַרְעוֹ חָלָל, וְהוּא אֵינוֹ חָלָל:


כהן אינו מתחלל – if he married a divorcee, the priest’s wife’s illegitimately married to him or a harlot, but rather all the while that he is with her, he is unfit for Divine service. When he divorces her, he returns to his state of spiritual fitness, as it is written (Leviticus 21:15): “that he may not profane his offspring among his kin;” his progeny are profaned but he is not is not profaned.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:7:5)


**כֹּהֶנֶת מִטַּמְּאָה לְמֵתִים.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן, וְלֹא בְנוֹת אַהֲרֹן:


כהנת מטמאה למתים – as it is written (Leviticus 21:1): “[Speak to the priests,] the sons of Aaron,” and not the daughters of Aaron.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:7:6)


**וְאֵין כֹּהֶנֶת אוֹכֶלֶת בְּקָדְשֵׁי קָדָשִׁים.** דְּחַטָּאת וְאָשָׁם וּמִנְחָה בְּכוּלְהוּ כְּתִיב (שם ו) כָּל זָכָר בִּבְנֵי אַהֲרֹן יֹאכְלֶנוּ:


ואין כהנת אוכלת בקדשי קדשים – for the sin offering and guilt offering and meal-offering, with all of them it is written (Leviticus 6:11): “Only the males among Aaron’s descendants shall eat of it.”



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:8:1)


**הָאִישׁ פּוֹרֵעַ וּפוֹרֵם.** כְּשֶׁהוּא מְנֻגַּע פּוֹרֵעַ אֶת רֹאשׁוֹ וּפוֹרֵם אֶת בְּגָדָיו, דִּכְתִיב (שם יג) אִישׁ צָרוּעַ הוּא וְכוּ', אִישׁ פּוֹרֵעַ וּפוֹרֵם, וְאֵין אִשָּׁה פּוֹרַעַת וּפוֹרֶמֶת:


האיש פורע ופורם – when he is afflicted with leprosy, he lets his hair grow and tears his garments, as it is written (Leviticus 13:44): “The man is leprous; he is unclean.” A man lets his hair grow and he tears his garments. A woman does not let her hair grow and she does not tear her garments.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:8:2)


**הָאִישׁ מַדִּיר בְּנוֹ בְּנָזִיר.** בְּנוֹ קָטָן. וְחָלָה נְזִירָתוֹ עָלָיו וַאֲפִלּוּ לִכְשֶׁיִּגְדַּל. וְזוֹ הֲלָכָה הִיא בַּנָּזִיר, וְאֵין לָהּ סֶמֶךְ מִן הַתּוֹרָה:


האיש מדיר בנו בנזיר – (this section of the Mishnah is also found in Tractate Nazir, Chapter 4, Mishnah 6) – His minor son and his Naziriteship falls upon him, and even when he grows up, and this Halakha is in [Tractate] Nazir and it has no support from the Torah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:8:3)


**הָאִישׁ מְגַלֵּחַ עַל נְזִירוּת אָבִיו.** אִם נָדַר אָבִיו בִּנְזִירוּת וְהִפְרִישׁ קָרְבְּנוֹתָיו וּמֵת וְהָיָה בְּנוֹ נָזִיר, אוֹ שֶׁנָּדַר בִּנְזִירוּת לְאַחַר מִיתַת אָבִיו, הֲרֵי הַבֵּן מְגַלֵּחַ בְּיוֹם מְלֹאֹת וּמֵבִיא קָרְבָּנוֹת שֶׁהִפְרִישׁ אָבִיו. וְגַם זוֹ הֲלָכָה וְאֵין לָהּ סֶמֶךְ:


האיש מגלח על נזירו אביו – (this section of the Mishnah is also found in Tractate Nazir, Chapter 5, Mishnah 7) – if his father made a vow in his Naziriteship and separated out his sacrifice (i.e., hair offering), and died, and his son was a Nazir, or that he took the vow of Naziriteship after the death of his father, the son shaves his head on the day of his fulfillment [of the vow] and he brings the sacrifices that his father separated out. And also this Halakha has no support.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:8:4)


**הָאִישׁ מְקַדֵּשׁ אֶת בִּתּוֹ.** מְקַבֵּל קִדּוּשֵׁי בִּתּוֹ קְטַנָּה שֶׁלֹּא מִדַּעְתָּהּ, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כב) אֶת בִּתִּי נָתַתִּי לָאִישׁ הַזֶּה:


האיש מקדש את בתו – (this section of the Mishnah is also found in Tractate Kiddushin, Chapter 2, Mishnah 1) – he accepts the betrothal of his minor daughter against her will, as it is written (Deuteronomy 22:16): “I gave this man my daughter to wife.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:8:5)


**הָאִישׁ נִסְקָל עָרֹם.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כד) וְרָגְמוּ אוֹתוֹ, וְאִי אֶפְשָׁר לוֹמַר אוֹתוֹ וְלֹא אוֹתָהּ, דְּהָא כְּתִיב (דברים יז) וְהוֹצֵאתָ אֶת הָאִישׁ הַהוּא אוֹ אֶת הָאִשָּׁה הַהִיא, אֶלָּא אוֹתוֹ בְּלֹא כְּסוּתוֹ אֲבָל אוֹתָהּ בִּכְסוּתָהּ:


האיש נסקל ערום – (this section of the Mishnah is also found in Tractate Sanhedrin, Chapter 6, Mishnayot 3-4) – as it is written (Leviticus 24:14): “and let [the whole community] stone him,” but it is impossible to state him and not her, for behold it is written (Deuteronomy 17:5): “you shall take the man or the woman [who did that wicked thing] out [to the public place],” but rather, him but not his clothing, but her (i.e., this woman) with her clothes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:8:6)


**הָאִישׁ נִתְלֶה.** דִּכְתִיב (שם כא) וְתָלִיתָ אוֹתוֹ עַל עֵץ, אוֹתוֹ וְלֹא אוֹתָהּ:


האיש נתלה – as it is written (Deuteronomy 21:22): “and you impale him on a stake,” him and not her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 3:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/3:8:7)


**הָאִישׁ נִמְכָּר בִּגְנֵבָתוֹ.** דִּכְתִיב (שמות כב) וְנִמְכַּר בִּגְנֵבָתוֹ, וְלֹא בִגְנֵבָתָהּ:


האיש נמכר בגניבתו – as it is written (Exodus 22:2): “he shall be sold for his theft,” and not her theft.



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:1:1)


**אֲרוּסָה וְשׁוֹמֶרֶת יָבָם.** אֲרוּסָה שֶׁקִּנֵּא לָהּ אָרוּס וְשׁוֹמֶרֶת יָבָם שֶׁקִּנֵּא לָהּ יָבָם:


ארוסה ושומרת יבם – a betrothed woman whose betrothed is jealous of her and the childless brother’s widow awaiting levirate marriage whose levir is jealous of her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:1:2)


**לֹא שׁוֹתוֹת.** דְּמַעֲטִינְהוּ קְרָא:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:1:3)


**וְלֹא נוֹטְלוֹת כְּתֻבָּה.** שֶׁהִיא גָרְמָה לֶאֱסֹר עַצְמָהּ עָלָיו, שֶׁנִּסְתְּרָה אַחַר קִנּוּי, דְּמִקִּנּוּי וְאִסּוּר לֹא אִמְעִיט, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר ה) דַּבֵּר אֶל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל כוּ', לְרַבּוֹת אֲרוּסָה וְשׁוֹמֶרֶת יָבָם לְקִנּוּי:


ולא נוטלות כתובה – for she caused to make herself prohibited upon him for she had retired under suspicious circumstances after a warning given to the suspected wife–manifestation of jealousy that from this manifestation of jealousy and the prohibition, one does not do less, as it is written (Numbers 5:12): “Speak to the Israelite people, etc.” which includes the betrothed woman and the childless brother’s widow awaiting levirate marriage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:1:4)


**תַּחַת אִישָׁהּ.** בְּיוֹשֶׁבֶת תַּחְתָּיו הַכָּתוּב מְדַבֵּר:


תחת אישה – The verse states that she sits underneath him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:1:5)


**נְתִינָה.** מִן הַגִּבְעוֹנִים. וַאֲסוּרִים לָבֹא בַּקָּהָל:


נתינה – from the Gibeonites and they are prohibited from entering the congregation [of the Lord].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:1:6)


**לֹא שׁוֹתוֹת.** דְּלֹא נֶאֶמְרָה פָּרָשָׁה אֶלָּא בָּרְאוּיָה לְקַיְּמָהּ, דִּכְתִיב (שם) כִּי תִשְׂטֶה אִשְׁתּוֹ, בָּרְאוּיָה לְאִישׁוּת הַכָּתוּב מְדַבֵּר, פְּרָט לְאַלְמָנָה לְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל וְכוּ':


לא שותות – this portion was not stated other than when it was appropriate to fulfill it, as it is written (Numbers 5:12): “if any man’s wife has gone astray.” The Biblical verse speaks of what is appropriate to marriage, except for a widow [married] with a High Priest [or a divorcee or a woman released from a levirate marriage by Halitzah with a regular Kohen,] etc.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:1:7
