[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:1:7)


**וְלֹא נוֹטְלוֹת כְּתֻבָּה.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּשְׁאָר אַלְמָנוֹת לְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל גּוֹבוֹת כְּתֻבָּתָן כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בִּכְתֻבּוֹת [ק: **וְכֵן בִּיבָמוֹת פד.**], זוֹ אֵין לָהּ כְּתֻבָּה, שֶׁסְּתִירָתָהּ גָּרְמָה לָהּ:


ולא נוטלות כתובה – even though the rest of the widows with a Kohen Gadol–High Priest collect their Ketubot, as we state in [Tractate] Ketubot [100b and Tractate Yevamot 84a]. This woman does not have her Ketubah because her retiring under suspicious circumstances caused this to her.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:2:1)


**אָמַר בַּעְלָהּ אֵינִי מַשְׁקָהּ וְשֶׁבַּעְלָהּ בָּא עָלֶיהָ בַדֶּרֶךְ.** שֶׁהוּא גוֹרֵם לָהּ שֶׁלֹּא תִשְׁתֶּה, נוֹטֶלֶת כְּתֻבָּתָהּ:


אמר בעלה איני מקשה ושבעלה בא עליה בדרך – since he causes her that she should not drink [the waters of bitterness], she takes–receives her Ketubah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:2:2)


**מֵתוּ בַעֲלֵיהֶן.** שֶׁל כָּל סוֹטוֹת הָרְאוּיוֹת לִשְׁתּוֹת, וְלֹא הִסְפִּיקוּ לְהַשְׁקוֹתָן עַד שֶׁמֵּתוּ:


מתו בעליהן – all of the women suspected of infidelity who are appropriate to drink, but did not have an opportunity to be caused [by their husbands] to drink [the bitter waters] until they died.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:2:3)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים נוֹטְלוֹת כְּתֻבָּה.** דִּשְׁטָר הָעוֹמֵד לִגָּבוֹת כְּגָבוּי דָּמֵי:


ב"ש אומרים נוטלות כתובה – for a document that is designated for collection is considered as collected.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:2:4)


**וְלֹא שׁוֹתוֹת.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְהֵבִיא הָאִישׁ אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ:


ולא שותות – as it is written (there – Numbers 5:15): “The man shall bring his wife [to the priest].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:2:5)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים לֹא שׁוֹתוֹת וְלֹא נוֹטְלוֹת כְּתֻבָּתָן.** כְּלוֹמַר מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁלֹּא שׁוֹתוֹת אֵין נוֹטְלוֹת כְּתֻבָּתָן, דִּשְׁטָר הָעוֹמֵד לִגָּבוֹת לָאו כְּגָבוּי דָּמֵי:


וב"ה אומרים לא שותות ולא נוטלות כתובתן – meaning to say that from out of the fact that they don’t drink [the waters of bitterness] , they don’t take their Ketubot, for the document that is designated for collection is not considered as already collected.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:3:1)


**מְעֻבֶּרֶת חֲבֵרוֹ.** שֶׁמֵּת אוֹ גֵּרְשָׁהּ וְהִנִּיחָהּ מְעֻבֶּרֶת אוֹ מֵנִיקָה. וַאֲסָרוּהָ חֲכָמִים לִנָּשֵׂא עַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַוָּלָד בֶּן שְׁתֵּי שָׁנִים. וְהָלַךְ זֶה וּנְשָׂאָהּ קֹדֶם הַזְּמַן, וְקִנֵּא לָהּ וְנִסְתְּרָה:


מעוברת חבירו – who died or divorced her and left her pregnant or nursing and the Sages forbid her to marry until the child born would be two years old and he went and married her prior to that time and was jealous of her and she had retired under suspicious circumstances.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:3:2)


**לֹא שׁוֹתָה.** שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה לְקַיְּמָהּ, דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי מֵאִיר הַנּוֹשֵׂא מְעֻבֶּרֶת חֲבֵרוֹ אוֹ מֵינֶקֶת חֲבֵרוֹ יוֹצִיא וְלֹא יַחְזִיר עוֹלָמִית. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


לא שותה – for she is not appropriate to fulfill it for Rabbi Meir holds ha a person who marries a woman who was impregnated by another or who had her become a nursing mother from another should divorce her and never ever return her. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:3:3)


**יָכוֹל הוּא לְהַפְרִישָׁהּ.** עַד עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה חֳדָשִׁים וּלְהַחְזִירָהּ, הִלְכָּךְ רְאוּיָה לְאִישׁוּת קָרִינָא בָּהּ:


יכול הוא להפרישה – until twenty-four months [have passed] and then to retore her. Therefore, we can her worthy of marriage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:3:4)


**אַיְלוֹנִית.** דּוּכְרָנִיתָא דְּלֹא יָלְדָה:


אילונית – she is man-like and does not give birth (i.e., barren, incapable of conception – see Talmud Ketubot 11a for its etymology from "איל"–ram-like).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:3:5)


**וְשֶׁאֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה לֵילֵד.** שֶׁשָּׁתְתָה כּוֹס עֲקָרִין. וְאָסוּר לְקַיְּמָן לְמִי שֶׁאֵין לוֹ בָנִים:


ושאינה ראויה לילד – for she had drank that cup of impotence–sterility, and it is prohibited to establish them for one who has not children.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:3:6)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר יָכוֹל הוּא לִשָּׂא אִשָּׁה אַחֶרֶת.** וְיִהְיֶה מֻתָּר לְקַיֵּם אֶת זֹאת, הִלְכָּךְ רְאוּיָה לְאִישׁוּת הִיא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


ר"א אומר יכול הוא לישא אשה אחרת – and he will be permitted to uphold this one. Therefore, she is appropriate for marriage. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:4:1)


**אֵשֶׁת כֹּהֵן שׁוֹתָה וּמֻתֶּרֶת לְבַעְלָהּ.** אִם נִמְצֵאת נְקִיָּה. דְּמַהוּ דְּתֵימָא הוֹאִיל וְאֵשֶׁת כֹּהֵן שֶׁנֶּאֶנְסָה אֲסוּרָה לְבַעְלָהּ, נָחוּשׁ לָהּ שֶׁמָּא נֶאֶנְסָה וּלְכָךְ לֹא בָּדְקוּהָ הַמַּיִם, קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן:


אשת כהן שותה ומותרת לבעלה – if she is found innocent, for you might have thought for since she is the wife of a Koehn that was forced [to have sexual intercourse with another man], she is forbidden to her husband, let us fear lest she was forced to have sexual intercourse [with another man] and therefore, the [bitter] waters did not examine her, this comes to teach us that this is not the case.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:4:2)


**אֵשֶׁת סָרִיס שׁוֹתָה.** דְּלֹא תֵימָא מִבִּלְעֲדֵי אִישֵׁךְ אָמַר רַחֲמָנָא, וְהַאי לָאו בַּר הָכִי הוּא, קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן:


אשת סריס – that you should not say that apart from “your wife” (Numbers 5:19), the All-Merciful said. But this one is not son of that one. This is what it comes to tell us.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:4:3)


**עַל יְדֵי כָל הָעֲרָיוֹת מְקַנְּאִים.** אִם קִנֵּא לָהּ מֵאָבִיהָ אוֹ מֵאָחִיהָ וּמֵאֶחָד מִכָּל עֲרָיוֹת, וְנִסְתְּרָה, הָוֵי קִנּוּי וְנֶאֱסֶרֶת עָלָיו וְשׁוֹתָה. דְּלֹא תֵימָא נִטְמְאָה נִטְמְאָה שְׁתֵּי פְּעָמִים, אֶחָד לַבַּעַל וְאֶחָד לַבּוֹעֵל, הֵיכָא דְמִתַּסְרָא עֲלֵיהּ בְּהַאי זְנוּת, לַאֲפוֹקֵי הָא דַאֲסִירָא עֲלֵיהּ וְקַיְמָא אֵימָא לֹא, קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן:


על ידי כל העריות מקנאים – if he was jealous of her from her father or from her brother, or from one of all of the prohibited relationships and she had retired under suspicious circumstances, it is a manifestation of warning and she is prohibited to him and she drinks [the waters of bitterness]. So that she will not state that “she was defiled,” “she was defiled,” twice – the first [time] to her husband and the [second] to the one who had engaged in intercourse with her, for where he is forbidden to her with this act of prostitution excluding that where is he is prohibited to her and we establish [stating not], this is what it comes to tell us [that this is not the case].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:4:4)


**חוּץ מִן הַקָּטָן.** פָּחוֹת מִבֶּן תֵּשַׁע שָׁנִים וְיוֹם אֶחָד, שֶׁאֵין בִּיאָתוֹ בִיאָה, כָּךְ פֵּרֵשׁ רַמְבַּ"ם. וְלִי נִרְאֶה קָטָן פָּחוֹת מִבֶּן שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה וְיוֹם אֶחָד וְלֹא הֵבִיא סִימָנִים, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר ה) וְשָׁכַב אִישׁ אוֹתָהּ, אִישׁ וְלֹא קָטָן:


חוץ מן הקטן – younger than nine years of age and one day where Maimonides explained that his coition is not coition. But to me, it appears that a minor child younger than age thirteen and one day who had not brought signs of puberty, as it is written (Numbers 5:13): “in that a man has had carnal relations with her,” a man, but not a minor child.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:4:5)


**וּמִמִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ אִישׁ.** לְמִעוּטֵי בְהֵמָה:


ממי שאינו איש – excluding an animal.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:5:1)


**אֵלּוּ שֶׁבֵּית דִּין מְקַנְּאִין לָהֶם.** אִם רוֹאִים אוֹתָם שֶׁמִּתְנַהֲגוֹת בִּפְרִיצוּת:


אלו שב"ד מקנאין להם – if they see them that they are acting with licentiousness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/4:5:2)


**וְלֹא לְהַשְׁקוֹתָהּ אָמְרוּ.** דְּהָא בָּעִינַן וְהֵבִיא הָאִישׁ אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ, וְלֵיכָּא. וּבַעְלָהּ נַמִּי לִכְשֶׁיָּבֹא אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְהַשְׁקוֹתָהּ בְּקִנּוּי זֶה, דְּבָעִינַן וְקִנֵּא וְהֵבִיא. וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי לֹא בָּעֵי וְקִנֵּא וְהֵבִיא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי. וְסוּמָא אֵינוֹ מַשְׁקֶה אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שם) מֵעֵינֵי אִישָׁהּ. וּמִמַּה שֶּׁנֶּאֱמַר (שם) אִשָּׁה תַּחַת אִישָׁהּ, לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁכָּל הַמּוֹנֵעַ הַבַּעַל מִלְּהַשְׁקוֹת מוֹנֵעַ הָאִשָּׁה מִלִּשְׁתּוֹת אִם הָיָה אוֹתוֹ הַדָּבָר בָּאִשָּׁה, לְפִיכָךְ אִם הָיְתָה הִיא סוּמָא אֵינָהּ שׁוֹתָה. וְאִם הָיְתָה חִגֶּרֶת אֵינָהּ שׁוֹתָה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שם) וְהֶעֱמִיד אוֹתָהּ הַכֹּהֵן. וְכֵן אִם הָיְתָה גִדֶּמֶת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שם) וְנָתַן עַל כַּפֶּיהָ. אוֹ אִלֶּמֶת, דְּבָעִינַן וְאָמְרָה הָאִשָּׁה. וְכֵן אִם הָיָה הַבַּעַל חִגֵּר אוֹ גִּדֵּם אוֹ אִלֵּם אֵינוֹ מַשְׁקֶה מִדִּכְתִיב אִשָּׁה תַּחַת אִישָׁהּ כִּדְאָמְרָן:


ולא להשקותה אמרו – for we surely require (Numbers 5:15): “that man shall bring his wife [to the priest]” and we don’t have it, and her husband also, when he will come, he cannot cause her to drink [the waters of bitterness] through this warning given to the suspected wife for we require (Numbers 5:14): “and he is wrought up about the wife [who has defiled herself]” and (Numbers 5:15): “that man shall bring his wife,” and Rabbi Yosi does not require: “he is wrought up about the wife”–"וקנא" and “that man shall bring his wife”–"והביא" and the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yosi. But a blind person does not cause his wife to drink, as it states (there – Numbers 5:13): “unbeknown to her husband”–"מעיני אישה" (literally, from the eyes of her husband), and from what is stated (Numbers 5:29): “a woman who goes astray while married to her husband”–"אשה תחת אישה" – we learned that whatever prevents the husband from causing [her] to drink, prevents the woman from drinking [the waters of bitterness] if his wife had the same thing. Therefore, if she was blind, she does not drink, and if she was lame, she does not drink, as it states (Numbers 5:18): “After he (i.e., the priest) has made the woman stand [before the LORD],” and similarly, if she had her hand or fingers cut off, as it states (Numbers 5:18): “and place upon her hands [the meal offering of remembrance],” or mute, as we require (Numbers 5:22): “And the woman shall say, [“Amen, amen],” and similarly, if the husband was lame, or had his fingers or hand cut off or was mute, he does not cause her to drink [the waters of bitterness, since it is written (Numbers 5:29): “when a woman goes astray while married to her husband”–"אשה תחת אישה" – as we have stated.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:1:1)


**כָּךְ בּוֹדְקִים אוֹתוֹ.** אֶת הַבּוֹעֵל:


כך בודקין – adulterer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:1:2)


**וּבָאוּ וּבָאוּ.** שְׁנֵיהֶם בְּוָי"ו יְתֵרָא לִדְרָשָׁא:


ובאו ובאו – both of them (Numbers 5:22 and Numbers 5:27) have an extra [letter] “vav” for interpretation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:1:3)


**נִטְמְאָה וְנִטְמָאָה.** וָי"ו יְתֵרָא דִּבְוְנִטְמְאָה שְׁנִיָּה לִדְרָשָׁא:


נטמאה ונטמאה – (Numbers 5:27 and Numbers 5:29) the extra “vav” in the second word "ונטמאה" –and defiles herself (Numbers 5:29) is for interpretation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:1:4)


**רַבִּי אוֹמֵר שְׁנֵי פְעָמִים וְכוּ'.** רַבִּי לֹא דָרֵישׁ וָי"ו יְתֵרָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אֶלָּא רִבּוּי דִקְרָאֵי קָא דָרֵישׁ, בֵּין בְּוּבָאוּ וּבָאוּ, בֵּין בְּנִטְמְאָה וְנִטְמָאָה:


רבי אומר שני פעמים וכו' – Rebbe (i.e., Rabbi Judah the Prince) did not expound the extra letter “vav” as Rabbi Akiva but rather the inclusion of the Biblical verses he expounds whether the two forms of "ובאו" –”and they enter” (verse 22) and “the spell-inducing waters enter” or whether two forms of "ונטמאה" –”if she defiled herself” (verses 28 and 29).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:2:1)


**בּוֹ בַּיּוֹם דָּרַשׁ.** כָּל הֵיכִי דִּתְנִינַן בּוֹ בַיּוֹם, בַּיּוֹם שֶׁהוֹשִׁיבוּ אֶת רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בֶּן עֲזַרְיָה לִנְשִׂיאוּת, שֶׁנִּתְרַבּוּ הַתַּלְמִידִים, שֶׁנָּתְנוּ רְשׁוּת לַכֹּל לִכָּנֵס. וּמִשּׁוּם דְּהַךְ דְּרָשָׁה דְנִטְמְאָה וְנִטְמָאָה נַמִּי בּוֹ בַיּוֹם נִדְרְשָׁה, נַקְטִינְהוּ לְכָל הָנָךְ הָכָא:


בו ביום דרש – every place where it teaches: "בו ביום"–on that selfsame day is the day where they appointed Rabbi Eleazar ben Azariah to the presidency, when the [number of] students were increased, they gave permission to all to enter (see Talmud Berakhot 28a). [And because of] of this exposition of נטמאה ונטמאה–she defiled herself (Numbers 5:27); and defiles herself (Numbers 5:29), also was expounded on that selfsame day, we take it as a tradition for all of these here.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:2:2)


**כְּלִי חֶרֶס.** הוּא רִאשׁוֹן, שֶׁנִּטְמָא מִן הַשֶּׁרֶץ שֶׁהוּא אָב. וְהָאֹכֶל שֶׁבְּתוֹכוֹ שֵׁנִי, שֶׁטִּמְּאוֹ הַכְּלִי:


כלי חרס – it is a first level of ritual impurity which was defiled from the unclean reptile which is one of the direct causes of Levitical impurity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:2:3)


**אֵינוֹ אוֹמֵר טָמֵא.** כָּל אֲשֶׁר בְּתוֹכוֹ טָמֵא. אֶלָּא יִטְמָא, לְמִדְרָשׁ בֵּיהּ נַמִּי יְטַמֵּא, שֶׁמְּטַמֵּא אֶת אֲחֵרִים:


אינו אומר טמא – (Leviticus 11:33): “everything inside it shall be unclean”; it does not say טמא–is impure–unclean but rather יטמא–will be unclean–impure. It comes to expound also – that יטמא means – that it defiles other things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:2:4)


**לִמֵּד עַל כִּכָּר שֵׁנִי.** לְהָכִי נָקַט כִּכָּר, לְפִי שֶׁהוּא מָצוּי בַּתַּנּוּר:


למד על ככר שני – therefore it took the tradition of a loaf because it is found in an oven.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:2:5)


**שֶׁמְּטַמֵּא אֶת הַשְּׁלִישִׁי.** וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּחֻלִּין. דִּקְרָא סְתָמָא כְּתִיב:


שמטמא את השלישי – and even that which is non-holy, for the Biblical verse was written undefined.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:2:6)


**דּוֹר אַחֵר.** מִן הָעֲתִידִים לָבֹא. שֶׁיְּטַהֵר אֶת הַשְּׁלִישִׁי, וְאַף בִּתְרוּמָה, שֶׁאֵין לוֹ מִקְרָא מִן הַתּוֹרָה:


דור אחר – from those who will come in the future who will purify the third level of Levitical impurity, and even Terumah–priest’s due which has no Biblical verse regarding it from the Torah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:2:7)


**וַהֲרֵי עֲקִיבָא תַּלְמִידְךָ מֵבִיא לוֹ רְאָיָה מִן הַמִּקְרָא שֶׁהוּא טָמֵא.** אַף בְּחֻלִּין. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא, דְּאֵין שֵׁנִי עוֹשֶׂה שְׁלִישִׁי בְּחֻלִּין, אֶלָּא בִּתְרוּמָה:


והרי עקיבא תלמידך מביא לו ראיה מן המקרא שהוא טמא – even for non-holy produce, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Akiva and something that is second level of Levitical impurity does not make something third level of Levitical impurity for non-holy produce, but only for produce dedicated to the Temple.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:3:1)


**מִגְרָשׁ.** רְחָבָה פְּנוּיָה מִזְּרִיעָה וּמֵאִילָנוֹת לְנוֹי הָעִיר: **וְאַלְפַּיִם.** לֹא הֻזְכַּר לְתִתָּם לַלְוִיִּם, וְלֹא נֶאֶמְרוּ אֶלָּא לִיצִיאַת תְּחוּם שַׁבָּת:


מגרש – an open place free from sowing and from trees for the beauty of the city. But two-thousand [cubits] is not mentioned there for the Levites and is not mentioned other than [for leaving from the limits of] Shabbat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:3:2)


**שָׂדוֹת וּכְרָמִים.** וְאַלְפַּיִם סָבִיב נִתְּנוּ לַלְוִיִּם. מֵהֶם הִנִּיחוּ אֶלֶף סָבִיב הָעִיר לַמִּגְרָשׁ, וְהַשְּׁאָר שָׂדוֹת וּכְרָמִים. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בְּנוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי יוֹסֵי הַגְּלִילִי, שֶׁתְּחוּם אַלְפַּיִם אַמָּה דְּרַבָּנָן:


שדות וכרמים – and two-thousand [cubits] surrounding they gave to the Levites, and from them they left one-thousand [cubits] surrounding the city for the fields and the rest of the fields and vineyards. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Eliezer the son of Rabbi Yosi HaGlili that the Sabbath limit is two-thousand [cubits] according to the Rabbis.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:4:1)


**שֶׁאֵין תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר לֵאמֹר.** שֶׁאֵין דּוֹמֶה לִשְׁאָר לֵאמֹר שֶׁבַּתּוֹרָה, שֶׁהַשְּׁכִינָה מְדַבֶּרֶת לְמֹשֶׁה לֵאמֹר הַדִּבּוּר לְיִשְׂרָאֵל, אֲבָל כָּאן אֵין לוֹמַר כֵּן:


שאין ת"ל לאמר – which is not similar to the other [mentions] of [the word] "לאמר" –saying, that are in the Torah for the Divine Presence speaks to Moses stating the utterance to Israel. But here, we don’t say this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:4:2)


**כְּקוֹרִין אֶת הַהַלֵּל.** רָאשֵׁי פְרָקִים בִּלְבַד הָיוּ עוֹנִים אַחֲרָיו:


בקורין את ההלל – headings of chapters alone that they would answer following them.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:5:1)


**מֵאַהֲבָה.** שֶׁאָהַב אֶת הַמָּקוֹם:


מאהבה – that he loved “the place” (i.e., God).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:5:2)


**שָׁקוּל.** מִשְׁקָלוֹ שָׁוֶה, אֵינוֹ מַכְרִיעַ לְכָאן וּלְכָאן. יֵשׁ לוֹ הַכָּתוּב בְּוָי"ו וּמַשְׁמָעוּתוֹ כְאִלּוּ הָיָה לֹא בְאָלֶ"ף. וְיֵשׁ לֹא בְאָלֶ"ף וּמַשְׁמָעוּתוֹ כְּאִלּוּ כָּתוּב בְּוָי"ו. הֵן יִקְטְלֵנִי, הַלֹּא הוּא הוֹרְגֵנִי, לֹא אֲיַחֵל לוֹ עוֹד. אוֹ לוֹ אֲנִי מְיַחֵל:


שקול – the weight will be equivalent, not outweighing to one side or the other, and he holds the Biblical verse with the letter “Vav,” and its implication is that the Biblical verse ended with a “Vav.” "הן יקטלני"–He may well slay me (Job 13:15) – though he kills me, “yet I will trust in Him no longer,” or, “yet I will trust in Him.” (whether one reads the word as לא or לו ).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:5:3)


**מִיִּרְאָה.** מִדַּאֲגַת פֻּרְעָנוּת שֶׁלֹּא תָבֹא עָלָיו:


מיראה – from fear of retribution that will not come upon him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 5:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/5:5:4)


**יְרֵא אֱלֹהִים.** וְלֹא אוֹהֵב אֱלֹהִים:


ירא אלהים – and he doesn’t love God.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:1:1)


**מִי שֶׁקִּנֵּא לְאִשְׁתּוֹ וְנִסְתָּרָה אֲפִלּוּ שָׁמַע מֵעוֹף הַפּוֹרֵחַ.** שֶׁנִּסְתְּרָה:


משקינא לה ונסתרה אפילו שמע מעף הפורח that she retired under suspicious circumstances–secluded herself,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:1:2)


**יוֹצִיא וְיִתֵּן כְּתֻבָּה.** רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר לְטַעֲמֵיהּ דְּאָמַר בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא סְתִירָה לֹא בַּעְיָא עֵדוּת, וַאֲפִלּוּ עֶבֶד וַאֲפִלּוּ שִׁפְחָה נֶאֱמָנִים, דְּהַיְנוּ נַמִּי כְּעוֹף הַפּוֹרֵחַ, דְּאִתַּקַּשׁ סְתִירָה לְטֻמְאָה דְּכֻלְּהוּ מְהֵימוֹנֵי בָהּ, הִלְכָּךְ נֶאֶסְרָה עָלָיו. וְאִם אֵינוֹ רוֹצֶה לְהַשְׁקוֹתָהּ יוֹצִיא וְיִתֵּן כְּתֻבָּה:


יוציא ויתן כתובה – he should divorce her and provide her Ketubah settlement. Rabbi Eliezer, according to his reasoning who said in the first chapter (Tractate Sotah, Chapter 1, Mishnah 1), that a married woman’s retirement with a man under suspicious circumstances does not require testimony and even a slave and even a maid-servant are believed, that is (identical with) a flying bird for a married woman’s retirement with a man under suspicious circumstances is compared through close analogy of Biblical verses to ritual defilement and everyone is believed regarding her. Therefore, if she retired with a man under suspicious circumstances and he does not want to have to cause her to drink [the bitter waters], he should divorce her and provide her with her Ketubah settlement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:1:3)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר עַד שֶׁיִּשְׂאוּ וְיִתְּנוּ בָהּ מוֹזְרוֹת בַּלְּבָנָה.** נָשִׁים הַטֹּווֹת לְאוֹר הַלְּבָנָה. רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ לְטַעְמֵיהּ דְּאָמַר מַשְׁקֶה עַל פִּי שְׁנַיִם. מִיהוּ מִשֶּׁיִּשְּׂאוּ וְיִתְּנוּ בָּהּ מוֹזְרוֹת בַּלְּבָנָה בִּפְרִיצוּתָהּ מְכֹעָר הַדָּבָר וְתֵצֵא, דְּהָא אֲפִלּוּ מַיָּא תּוּ לֹא בָּדְקֵי לָהּ כִּדְתַנְיָא בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא. וּטְהוֹרָה הִיא. וְלֹא שֶׁיִּשְּׂאוּ וְיִתְּנוּ בָּהּ מוֹזְרוֹת בַּלְּבָנָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


רבי יהושע אומר עד שישאו ויתנו בה מוזרות בלבנה – women who spin by the light of the moon. Rabbi Yehoshua, according to his reasoning, who said that we cause her to drink [by the testimony] of two [witnesses]. However, when they (i.e., the women twisting yarn) speak of her by [the light of] the moon, the matter is ugly licentiousness, and she should be divorced for even water, moreover, does not examine her as it is taught in the first chapter. But she is ritually pure, and women twisting yarn by moonlight should be talking about her. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:2:1)


**אֲנִי רְאִיתִיהָ שֶׁנִּטְמֵאת.** בְּאוֹתָהּ סְתִירָה, שֶׁהִיא עַל פִּי עֵדִים לְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ, אוֹ עַל פִּי עוֹף הַפּוֹרֵחַ לְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


אני ראיתיה שנטמאת – in that retirement [of hers] under suspicious circumstances which is through two witnesses according to Rabbi Yehoshua or through the flying bird according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:2:2)


**לֹא הָיְתָה שׁוֹתָה.** דְּעֵד אֶחָד נֶאֱמָן בָּהּ אַף לְהַפְסִידָהּ כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, שֶׁרַגְלַיִם לַדָּבָר:


לא היתה שותה – for one witness is believed concerning her even to cause her to lose her Ketubah settlement for there is a basis for it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:2:3)


**אַף לְפָסְלָהּ מִכְּתֻבָּתָהּ.** שֶׁלֹּא תִשְׁתֶּה וְלֹא תִטֹּל כְּתֻבָּתָהּ:


אף לפסלה מכתובתה – that she should not drink [the bitter waters] not take [the settlement of] her Ketubah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:2:4)


**יְבִמְתָּהּ.** אֵשֶׁת אֲחִי בַּעְלָהּ:


יבמתה – the wife of her husband’s brother.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:2:5)


**וּבַת בַּעְלָהּ.** מֵאִשָּׁה אַחֶרֶת:


ובת בעלה – from another wife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:2:6)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ נֶאֱמָנוֹת.** לְעֵדוּת זוֹ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵן פְּסוּלוֹת לְכָל שְׁאָר עֵדוּת שֶׁהוּא לְרָעָתָהּ, לְפִי שֶׁהַתּוֹרָה הֶאֱמִינָה כָּל עֵדוּת שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ. וּמִיהוּ לֹא לְפָסְלָהּ מִכְּתֻבָּתָהּ, הוֹאִיל וְשׂוֹנְאוֹת אוֹתָהּ, אֶלָּא שֶׁלֹּא תִשְׁתֶּה:


הרי אלו נאמנות – for this testimony, even though they are ineligible for all other testimony which is to her detriment because the Torah believed all testimony that there is concerning her. But, however, not to make her ineligible for her Ketubah [settlement], for they hate her, but rather, that she should not be suspected by her husband of having been unfaithful.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:3:1)


**שֶׁהָיָה בְדִין.** שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא נֶאֱמָן בָּהּ עֵד אֶחָד, אִם לֹא מִגְּזֵרַת הַכָּתוּב:


שהיה בדין – that one witness should not be believed concerning her if it is not from the decree of the Biblical verse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:3:2)


**עֵדוּת הָרִאשׁוֹנָה.** סְתִירָה:


עדות הראשונה – her retirement under suspicious circumstances which does not make her forbidden [to her husband] eternally other than until she drinks [the bitter waters].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:3:3)


**שֶׁאֵין אוֹסַרְתָּהּ אִסּוּר עוֹלָם.** אֶלָּא עַד שֶׁתִּשְׁתֶּה:


אינה מתקיימת בפחות משנים – since we require to state from an analogy–Gezerah Shavah, and this is Rabbi Yehoshua’s view.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:3:4)


**אֵינָהּ מִתְקַיֶּמֶת בְּפָחוֹת מִשְּׁנַיִם.** כִּדְבָעִינַן לְמֵימַר מִגְּזֵרָה שָׁוָה, וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ הִיא:


עדות האחרונה – ritual impurity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:3:5)


**עֵדוּת הָאַחֲרוֹנָה.** טֻמְאָה:


כל [עדות] שיש בה – it is believed concerning her, as it is written (Numbers 5:13): “and there is no witness against her,” and all mentions of the word "עד"–witness that is mentioned in the Torah, undefined, is not other than two, as it necessitated the Bible to state (Deuteronomy 19:15): “A single witness may not validate against a person [any guilt or blame for any offense that may be committed: a case can be valid only on the testimony of two witnesses or more].” We learn from this that "עד"–witness implies two [witnesses], until the Biblical verse explicitly specifies one [witness]. And as it states, that she was not caught to be forbidden, so we see that one witness is believed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:3:6)


**כָּל עֵדוּת שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ.** נֶאֱמָן בָּהּ, דִּכְתִיב וְעֵד אֵין בָּהּ, וְכָל עֵד הָאָמוּר בַּתּוֹרָה סְתָם אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא שְׁנַיִם, מִדְּאִצְטְרִיךְ קְרָא לוֹמַר (דברים יט) לֹא יָקוּם עֵד אֶחָד בְּאִישׁ, שְׁמַע מִנָּהּ דְּעֵד שְׁנַיִם בַּמַּשְׁמָע, עַד שֶׁיִּפְרֹט לְךָ הַכָּתוּב אֶחָד. וְהָכִי קָאָמַר קְרָא, תְּרֵי לֵית בָּהּ אֶלָּא חַד, וְקָאָמַר וְהִיא לֹא נִתְפָּסָה, לְאוֹסְרָהּ, אַלְמָא עֵד אֶחָד מְהֵימָן:



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:4:1)


**עֵד אוֹמֵר נִטְמֵאת וְעֵד אוֹמֵר לֹא נִטְמֵאת.** אוֹקֵי חַד לַהֲדֵי חַד, וַהֲרֵי הִיא בִּסְפֵקָה וְשׁוֹתָה. וְדַוְקָא כִּי אָתוּ בַּהֲדֵי הֲדָדֵי, אֲבָל בָּזֶה אַחַר זֶה, כְּשֶׁאָמַר הָרִאשׁוֹן נִטְמֵאת, הָוֵי מְהֵימָן כְּבִתְרֵי, וְהַשֵּׁנִי הָאוֹמֵר לֹא נִטְמֵאת הָוֵי לֵיהּ חַד, וְאֵין דְּבָרָיו שֶׁל אֶחָד בִּמְקוֹם שְׁנַיִם:


עד אומר נטמאת ועד אומר לא נטמאת – establish one near one, and there is doubt regarding her and she drinks, and especially when they come one in the presence of the other. But this one after that one, when the first stated that she was defiled, he is believed as two [witnesses]. But the second who states that was not defiled, is just considered as one, for the words of the one are not in place of two [witnesses].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/6:4:2)


**וּשְׁנַיִם אוֹמְרִים לֹא נִטְמֵאת.** כְּלוֹמַר לֹא נִטְמֵאת בְּפָנֶיךָ, שֶׁכְּשֶׁבָּאתָ וּמָצָאת שֶׁנִּסְתְּרוּ יַחַד גַּם אָנוּ הָיִינוּ עִמְּךָ וּבְפָנֵינוּ לֹא נִטְמֵאת, הֲרֵי נִסְתַּלֵּק הָעֵד וּבָטְלוּ דְּבָרָיו מִפְּנֵי הַשְּׁנַיִם, וַהֲרֵי הִיא בִּסְפֵקָה אִם נִטְמֵאת קֹדֶם שֶׁבָּאוּ אֵלּוּ וּמְצָאוּם, וּלְפִיכָךְ שׁוֹתָה. וְאַשְׁמוּעִינַן תַּנָּא בְּהָנֵי תְּרֵי בָּבֵי דְּמַתְנִיתִין דְּבִפְסוּלֵי עֵדוּת הֲלֵךְ אַחַר רֹב דֵּעוֹת בֵּין לְקֻלָּא בֵּין לְחֻמְרָא, כְּלוֹמַר בֵּין לְהַשְׁקוֹתָהּ בֵּין שֶׁלֹּא לְהַשְׁקוֹתָהּ:


ושנים אומרים לא נטמאת – meaning to say that she was not defiled in your presence, that when you came and found that they retired under suspicious circumstances together and we also were with you and in your presence she was not defiled, the one witness was dismissed and his words were voided because of the two [witnesses] and she remains in a state of “doubt” if she had been defiled before they came and found them [together] and therefore, she drinks [the bitter waters]. And we are taught by the Tanna–teacher in these two clauses of our Mishnah that they are ineligible for [providing] testimony, they went after the majority opinion whether for leniency or stringency, meaning to say, whether to cause her to drink or not to cause her to drink.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:1:1)


**אֵלּוּ נֶאֱמָרִין.** פָּרָשַׁת סוֹטָה. דִּכְתִיב (במדבר ה) וְאָמַר אֶל הָאִשָּׁה, בְּכָל לָשׁוֹן שֶׁהוּא אוֹמֵר:


אלו נאמרין. פרשת סוטה – as it is written (Numbers 5:19): “[The priest shall adjure the woman,] saying to her, [“If no man has lain with you, if you have not gone astray in defilement while married to your husband, be immune to harm from this water of bitterness that induces the spell].” In any language that he states it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:1:2)


**וּוִדּוּי מַעֲשֵׂר.** בִּעַרְתִּי הַקֹּדֶשׁ מִן הַבַּיִת כוּ', אוֹמֵר בְּכָל לָשׁוֹן שֶׁיִּרְצֶה, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כו) וְאָמַרְתָּ לִפְנֵי ה'‏ אֱלֹהֶיךָ, בְּכָל לָשׁוֹן שֶׁאַתָּה אוֹמֵר:


וידוי מעשר – (Deuteronomy 26:13): “I have cleared out the consecrated portion from the house, etc.” He states it in any language that he wishes, as it is written (ibid.): “You shall declare before the LORD your God,” in every language that he speaks.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:1:3)


**קְרִיאַת שְׁמַע.** דִּכְתִיב (שם ו) שְׁמַע יִשְׂרָאֵל, בְּכָל לָשׁוֹן שֶׁאַתָּה שׁוֹמֵעַ:


קריאת שמע – as it is written (Deuteronomy 6:4): “Hear O Israel ” in any language that you hear.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:1:4)


**וּתְפִלָּה.** צִבּוּר שֶׁמִּתְפַּלְּלִין, מִתְפַּלְּלִין בְּכָל לָשׁוֹן. אֲבָל יָחִיד אֵינוֹ מִתְפַּלֵּל אֶלָּא בִּלְשׁוֹן הַקֹּדֶשׁ:


ותפלה – a congregation–community that prays, prays–recites the Amidah in any language, but an individual does not pray other than in the holy tongue (i.e., Hebrew).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:1:5)


**וּבִרְכַּת הַמָּזוֹן.** דִּכְתִיב (שם ח) וּבֵרַכְתָּ, בְּכָל לָשׁוֹן שֶׁאַתָּה מְבָרֵךְ:


וברכת המזון – as it is written (Deuteronomy 8:10): ‘and you shall bless–give thanks [the LORD your God],” in any language that you bless–give thanks.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:1:6)


**וּשְׁבוּעַת הָעֵדוּת וּשְׁבוּעַת הַפִּקָּדוֹן.** בְּכָל לָשׁוֹן שֶׁהִשְׁבִּיעוּם וְלֹא הוֹדוּ, חַיָּבִין. שְׁבוּעַת הָעֵדוּת יָלְפִינַן לַהּ דִּכְתִיב בָּהּ (ויקרא ה) וְשָׁמְעָה קוֹל אָלָה, בְּכָל לָשׁוֹן שֶׁאַתָּה שׁוֹמֵעַ. וּשְׁבוּעַת הַפִּקָּדוֹן גָּמַר תֶּחֱטָא תֶחֱטָא מִשְּׁבוּעַת הָעֵדוּת:


ושבועת העדות ושבועת הפקדון – in any language that he made them take an oath and did not give thanks, they are liable. The oath of testimony, we derive it as it is written regarding it (Leviticus 5:1): “[If a person incurs guilt] when he heard a public imprecation,” and the oath of deposit, he derives it [through an analogy] from the oath of testimony (Tractate Sotah 33a): "תחטא" –incurs guilt (Leviticus 5:1) and "תחטא"–sins (Leviticus 5:21).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:2:1)


**מִקְרָא בִכּוּרִים.** שֶׁהָיָה קוֹרֵא מֵאֲרַמִּי אוֹבֵד אָבִי (דברים כו) עַד סוֹף הַפָּרָשָׁה:


מקרא בכורים – that he would recite from (Deuteronomy 26:5): “My father was a fugitive Aramean,” until the conclusion of the portion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:2:2)


**וַחֲלִיצַת יְבָמָה.** הִיא אוֹמֶרֶת (שם כה) מֵאֵן יְבָמִי לְהָקִים לְאָחִיו שֵׁם בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל לֹא אָבָה יַבְּמִי, וְהוּא אוֹמֵר לֹא חָפַצְתִּי לְקַחְתָּהּ, וְאַחַר הַחֲלִיצָה אוֹמֶרֶת כָּכָה יֵעָשֶׂה לָאִישׁ:


וחליצת יבמה – she states (Deuteronomy 25:7): “My husband’s brother refuses to establish a name in Israel for his brother,” and he states (Deuteronomy 25:8): “I do not want to marry her.” And after the ceremony of removing the shoe of the brother-in-law–levir, she says (Deuteronomy 25:9): “Thus shall be done to the man [who will not uphold his brother’s house!]”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:2:3)


**בְּרָכוֹת וּקְלָלוֹת.** שֶׁאָמְרוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּהַר גְּרִזִּים וּבְהַר עֵיבָל:


ברכות וקללות – that the Jewish people recited at Mount Gerizim and Mount Ebal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:2:4)


**בִּרְכַּת כֹּהֲנִים.** נְשִׂיאַת כַּפַּיִם:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:2:5)


**בִּרְכוֹת כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל.** בְּיוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים אַחַר עֲבוֹדַת הַיּוֹם שֶׁלִּפְנַי וְלִפְנִים הוּא קוֹרֵא אֶת הַפָּרָשָׁה בַּתּוֹרָה, וּמְבָרֵךְ שְׁמֹנֶה בְרָכוֹת הַמְּפֹרָשׁוֹת בְּמַתְנִיתִין:


ברכות כ"ג – after the Divine Service on the Day (i.e., Yom Kippur) in the innermost sanctum (i.e., the Holy of Holies), he would read the portion in the Torah and make the eight blessings that are explained in our Mishnah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:2:6)


**וּפָרָשַׁת הַמֶּלֶךְ.** הִיא פָּרָשַׁת הַקְהֵל, כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ בְּסוֹף פִּרְקִין:


ופרשת המלך – this is the portion of "הקהל"–Gathering the people (see Deuteronomy 31:10-13), as it explains at the end of our chapter (Mishnah 8).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:2:7)


**וּפָרָשַׁת עֶגְלָה עֲרוּפָה.** (שם כא) יָדֵינוּ לֹא שָׁפְכוּ אֶת הַדָּם הַזֶּה, כַּפֵּר לְעַמְּךָ יִשְׂרָאֵל וְגוֹ':


ופרשת עגלה ערופה – (Deuteronomy 21:7): “Our hands did not shed this blood,” (Deuteronomy 21:8): “Absolve, O LORD, your people Israel, etc.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:2:8)


**פָּרָשַׁת מְשׁוּחַ מִלְחָמָה.** (שם כ) וְהָיָה כְּקָרָבְכֶם וְגוֹ'‏ שְׁמַע יִשְׂרָאֵל אַתֶּם קְרֵבִים הַיּוֹם וְגוֹ':


פרשת משוח מלחמה – (Deuteronomy 20:2): “Before you join battle, etc,” (Deuteronomy 20:3): “Hear, O Israel! You are about to join battle [with your enemy].”



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:3:1)


**כֵּיצַד.** כְּמוֹ מִנַּיִן:


כיצד – like from where.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:3:2)


**וּלְהַלָּן הוּא אוֹמֵר.** בַּבְּרָכוֹת וּקְלָלוֹת שֶׁל הַר גְּרִזִּים:


ולהלן הוא אומר – in the blessings and curses of Har Gerizim (Deuteronomy 27:11-14).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:3:3)


**מָה עֲנִיָּה הָאֲמוּרָה לְהַלָּן בִּלְשׁוֹן הַקֹּדֶשׁ.** דִּכְתִיב (שם כז) וְאָמְרוּ אֶל כָּל אִישׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל קוֹל רָם, וּכְתִיב הָתָם (שמות יט) וְהָאֱלֹהִים יַעֲנֶנּוּ בְּקוֹל, מַה לְּהַלָּן בִּלְשׁוֹן הַקֹּדֶשׁ, אַף כָּאן בִּלְשׁוֹן הַקֹּדֶשׁ:


מה ענייה האמורה להלן בלשון הקודש – as it is written (Deuteronomy 27:14): “[The Levites] shall then proclaim in a loud voice to all the people of Israel.” And it is written there (Exodus 19:19): “God answered him in thunder.” Just as there, it is in the Holy Tongue (i.e., Hebrew), so here too, in the Holy Tongue.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:4:1)


**וְעָנְתָה וְאָמְרָה כָּכָה.** אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְלַמְּדָהּ בִּגְזֵרָה שָׁוָה, שֶׁמֵּעַצְמָהּ הִיא לְמֵדָה, וְעָנְתָה וְאָמְרָה כָּכָה, כַּלָּשׁוֹן הַזֶּה תֹּאמַר:


וענתה ואמרה ככה – there is no need to teach it through a Gezerah Shavah (analogy), for it is learned from itself, (Deuteronomy 25:9): ‘and [she shall] make this declaration: Thus [shall be done to the man who will not build up his brother’s house!].” In such a language she should state this.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:5:1)


**שֶׁבְּשׁוֹמְרוֹן.** אֵצֶל שׁוֹמְרוֹן:


שבשומרון – near Shomron–Samaria.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:5:2)


**מִכָּאן וּמִכָּאן.** עַל הֶהָרִים:


מכאן ומכאן – on the mountains.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:5:3)


**בְּשִׁבְעִים לָשׁוֹן.** בִּכְתָב שֶׁל שִׁבְעִים אֻמּוֹת, וְכָל הָרוֹצֶה לְלָמְדָהּ יָבֹא וְיִלְמֹד. שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיֶה פִּתְחוֹן פֶּה לָאֻמּוֹת לוֹמַר לֹא הָיָה לָנוּ מֵהֵיכָן לִלְמֹד:


בשבעים לשון – in the writing of seventy nations and all who wish to teach it should come to study so that there won’t be any fault-finding for the nations to state that we didn’t have from where to learn.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:5:4)


**אֶת הָאֲבָנִים.** סָתְרוּ אֶת הַמִּזְבֵּחַ לְאַחַר שֶׁהֶעֱלוּ הָעוֹלוֹת וְהַשְּׁלָמִים:


את האבנים – they concealed the alter after they offered the burnt offerings and the peace offerings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:5:5)


**וְלָנוּ בִמְקוֹמָן.** בְּבֵית מְלוֹנָם בַּגִּלְגָּל, וְשָׁם הֵקִימוּ אֶת הָאֲבָנִים:


ולנו במקומן – in their lodgings in Gilgal, and there, they established the stones.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:6:1)


**בַּמְּדִינָה אוֹמֵר אוֹתָהּ שָׁלֹשׁ בְּרָכוֹת.** שֶׁמַפְסִיקִין הַכֹּהֲנִים בֵּין פָּסוּק לְפָסוּק, וְעוֹנִים הַצִּבּוּר אָמֵן:


במדינה אומר אותה שלש ברכות – that the Kohanim interrupt between each verse, [and the community] answers [Amen].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:6:2)


**וּבַמִּקְדָּשׁ בְּרָכָה אֶחָת.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵין עוֹנִים אָמֵן בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ וְאֵין כָּאן שׁוּם הֶפְסֵק:


ובמקדש ברכה אחת – because they do not answer “Amen” in the Temple and there is no interruption here.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:6:3)


**כִּכְתָבוֹ.** בְּיוּ"ד הֵ"א:


ככתבה – with a Yud, Heih (i.e., the letters of the name of God).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:6:4)


**בְּכִנּוּיוֹ.** בְּאַלֶ"ף דָּלֶ"ת. שֶׁאֵין מַזְכִּירִין הַשֵּׁם כִּכְתָבוֹ אֶלָּא בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ בִּלְבַד, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שמות כ) בְּכָל הַמָּקוֹם אֲשֶׁר אַזְכִּיר אֶת שְׁמִי אָבֹא אֵלֶיךָ וּבֵרַכְתִּיךָ, סָרֵס הַמִּקְרָא וְדָרְשֵׁהוּ, בְּכָל הַמָּקוֹם אֲשֶׁר אָבֹא אֵלֶיךָ וּבֵרַכְתִּיךָ, דְּהַיְנוּ בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ, שָׁם אַזְכִּיר אֶת שְׁמִי:


בכנויו – with an Aleph, Daleth [Nun, Yud] for we don’t mention God’s name as it is written other than in the Temple alone, as it states (Exodus 20:21): “in every place where I cause My name to be mentioned, I will come to you and bless you.” The Biblical text transposed it and expounded upon it: In every place that I will come to you and bless you, which is in the Temple–Sanctuary, there I will mention My name.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:6:5)


**כְּנֶגֶד כִּתְפֵיהֶן.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁצָּרִיךְ נְשִׂיאַת כַּפַּיִם, דִּכְתִיב וַיִּשָּׂא אַהֲרֹן אֶת יָדָיו אֶל הָעָם וַיְבָרְכֵם, וּכְתִיב (דברים יח) הוּא וּבָנָיו כָּל הַיָּמִים, מָה הוּא בִּנְשִׂיאוּת כַּפַּיִם, אַף בָּנָיו בִּנְשִׂיאַת כַּפַּיִם כָּל הַיָּמִים:


כנגד כתפותיהם – because it requires the lifting of the palms of the hands, as it is written (Leviticus 9:22): “Aaron lifted up his hands toward the people and blessed them.” And it is written (Deuteronomy 18:5): “him and his descendants [to be in attendance for service in the name of the LORD] for all time.” Just as he is with the raising of his hands, so too his descendants–children also with the raising of their hands for all time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:6:6)


**וּבַמִּקְדָּשׁ.** שֶׁמְּבָרְכִים אֶת הָעָם בַּשֵּׁם הַמְּפֹרָשׁ וּשְׁכִינָה לְמַעְלָה מִקִּשְׁרֵי אֶצְבְּעוֹתֵיהֶם, מַגְבִּיהִים יְדֵיהֶם לְמַעְלָה מֵרָאשֵׁיהֶם:


ובמקדש – when they bless the people with the holy name of God and Divine Presence is above from the second joints of their fingers, they lift their hands above their heads.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:6:7)


**שֶׁאֵין מַגְבִּיהַּ יָדָיו לְמַעְלָה מִן הַצִּיץ.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַשֵּׁם כָּתוּב בּוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


שאין מגביה ידיו למעלה מן הציץ – because the name of God is written upon it. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:1)


**בִּרְכוֹת כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל.** בְּיוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים, לְאַחַר שֶׁכָּלְתָה עֲבוֹדַת הַיּוֹם קוֹרֵא בַתּוֹרָה וּמְבָרֵךְ שְׁמֹנֶה בְּרָכוֹת:


ברכות כ"ג – on Yom Kippur after the Divine Service–Avodah had been completed, he reads the Torah and makes eight blessings–benedictions.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:2)


**חַזַּן הַכְּנֶסֶת.** שַׁמַּשׁ הַכְּנֶסֶת שֶׁטֹּרַח עִסְקֵי הַכְּנֶסֶת עָלָיו לְהַכְנִיס וּלְהוֹצִיא לְהַפְשִׁיט אֶת הַתֵּבָה וּלְהָכִין [הַכֹּל]:


חזן הכנסת – the sexton of the assembly upon whom the duties of the assembly are upon him to bring in


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:3)


**רֹאשׁ הַכְּנֶסֶת.** עַל פִּיו נֶחְתָּכִין דִּבְרֵי הַכְּנֶסֶת, מִי יִפְרֹס עַל שְׁמַע, מִי יַפְטִיר בַּנָּבִיא, מִי יֵרֵד לִפְנֵי הַתֵּבָה:


ראש הכנסת – through his lips are decided all the matters of the congregation, who will recite the prayers leading up to the Shema for the benefit of those who come too late for the regular service, who will recite the Maftir–Haftarah from the prophets and who will go down before the Ark (to serve as the representative of the congregation).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:4)


**סְגָן.** הַמּוּכָן תַּחַת כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל. שֶׁאִם יֶאֱרַע בּוֹ פְּסוּל יְשַׁמֵּשׁ תַּחְתָּיו:


סגן – who is ready in place of the Kohen Gadol–High Priest for if something should happen to him to make him unfit for service, he will serve in his place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:5)


**וְהַסְּגָן לְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל.** וְכֻלָּן מִשּׁוּם כְּבוֹדוֹ שֶׁל כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל, דִּכְתִיב (משלי יד) בְּרָב עָם הַדְרַת מֶלֶךְ:


והסגן לכ"ג – and all of them are because of the honor of the High Priest, as it is written (Proverbs 14:28): “A numerous people is the glory of the king.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:6)


**וְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל עוֹמֵד וּמְקַבֵּל.** מִכְּלָל דְּיוֹשֵׁב הָיָה, שְׁמַע מִנָּהּ בְּעֶזְרַת נָשִׁים הָיָה קוֹרֵא. דְּאִי בְּעֶזְרַת יִשְׂרָאֵל קָיְמָא לָן אֵין יְשִׁיבָה בָּעֲזָרָה אֶלָּא לְמַלְכֵי בֵּית דָּוִד בִּלְבַד:


וכ"ג עומד ומקבל – it follows that he was sitting, we learn from this that he would read in the Women’s Court, for if it was in the Court of Israel, we hold that there is no sitting in that court other than only the kings of the house of David.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:7)


**וְקוֹרֵא אַחֲרֵי מוֹת.** שֶׁהוּא מִסֵּדֶר יוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים:


וקורא אחרי מות – which is the order of [the ritual of] Yom Kippur.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:8)


**וְאַךְ בֶּעָשׂוֹר.** שֶׁבְּפָרָשַׁת שׁוֹר אוֹ כֶּשֶׂב, דִּסְמוּכָה הִיא לְפָרָשַׁת אַחֲרֵי מוֹת וְיָכוֹל לִגְלֹל סֵפֶר תּוֹרָה לְשָׁם בְּעוֹד שֶׁהַמְּתֻרְגְּמָן מְתַרְגֵּם פָּסוּק אַחֲרוֹן שֶׁל פָּרָשַׁת אַחֲרֵי מוֹת, וְאֵין כָּאן שָׁהוּת כְּדֵי שֶׁיַּפְסִיק הַמְּתֻרְגְּמָן:


ואך בעשור – in the Torah portion (Leviticus 22:26 and following): “When an ox or a a sheep or a goat is born” that is near the portion of Aharei Mot (Leviticus 16) and he is able to roll the Torah scroll to there while the Aramaic translator is translating the concluding verse of Aharei Mot and there is no delay in order that the Aramaic translator should interrupt–stop.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:9)


**וְגוֹלֵל אֶת הַתּוֹרָה.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעֲדַיִן לֹא קָרָא בֶּעָשׂוֹר שֶׁל חֻמָּשׁ הַפְּקוּדִים, יִקְרָא אוֹתָהּ עַל פֶּה וְלֹא יִגְלֹל סֵפֶר תּוֹרָה לְשָׁם, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא רָחוֹק וְיֵשׁ שָׁהוּת שֶׁיַּפְסִיק הַתֻּרְגְּמָן וְאִיכָּא גְּנַאי לַצִּבּוּר. וְלִקְרוֹת בִּשְׁנֵי סִפְרֵי תּוֹרָה אִי אֶפְשָׁר, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין אָדָם אֶחָד קוֹרֵא בִּשְׁנֵי סִפְרֵי תּוֹרָה בַּצִּבּוּר, מִשּׁוּם פְּגָמוֹ שֶׁל סֵפֶר תּוֹרָה הָרִאשׁוֹן:


וגולל את התורה – and even though he did not read about the tenth [day of Tishrei] in the book of Numbers (chapter 29, verses 7-11), he reads it by heart. But he should not roll the Torah scroll to there because it is far and there is a delay that would cause the Aramaic translator to interrupt and it is a reproach to the community, and to read from two Torah scrolls is impossible because single person does not read from two Torah scrolls in the congregation because of the defect of the first Torah scroll.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:10)


**יוֹתֵר מִמַּה שֶּׁקָּרִיתִי לִפְנֵיכֶם.** מַה שֶּׁאֲנִי עָתִיד לִקְרוֹת עוֹד עַל פֶּה, אַל תֹּאמְרוּ שֶׁחָסֵר מִן הַסֵּפֶר הַזֶּה לְכָךְ אֵינִי גּוֹלְלוֹ שָׁם. וְכָל כָּךְ לָמָּה, שֶׁלֹּא לְהוֹצִיא לַעַז עַל סֵפֶר תּוֹרָה:


יותר ממה שקריתי לפניכם – what I am about to read further by heart, they should not say that it is missing from this Scroll, therefore, I won’t roll it there, and why so much? In order not to cast aspersion against the Torah scroll.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:11)


**עַל הַתּוֹרָה.** בְּרָכָה שֶׁלְּאַחֲרֶיהָ:


על התורה – the blessing that is after it (i.e., the Haftarah).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:12)


**וְעַל הָעֲבוֹדָה.** רְצֵה ה'‏ אֱלֹהֵינוּ:


ועל העבודה – May the LORD our God accept [our worship].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:13)


**וְעַל הַהוֹדָיָה.** מוֹדִים אֲנַחְנוּ לָךְ:


ועל ההודאה – We give thanks to You.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:14)


**וְעַל מְחִילַת עָוֹן.** אַתָּה בְחַרְתָּנוּ, שֶׁחוֹתֵם בָּהּ מֶלֶךְ מוֹחֵל וְסוֹלֵחַ לַעֲוֹנוֹתֵינוּ:


ועל מחילת עון – You have chosen us that we conclude it with “the king who pardons and forgives our iniquities.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:15)


**וְעַל הַמִּקְדָּשׁ.** שֶׁתִּשְׁרֶה שְׁכִינָה בְּבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ, וְחוֹתֵם בָּהּ בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה ה'‏ שׁוֹכֵן בְּצִיּוֹן:


ועל המקדש – that th Divine Presence should rest on the Temple and we conclude it with Praised is the LORD Who dwells in Zion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:16)


**וְעַל הַכֹּהֲנִים.** שֶׁיְּקֻבַּל קָרְבְּנוֹתֵיהֶם בְּרָצוֹן. וְחוֹתֵם בָּהּ מְקַדֵּשׁ הַכֹּהֲנִים:


ועל הכהנים – that He should accept their sacrifices with favor and we conclude it with [the blessing] “who sanctifies the Kohanim.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:7:17)


**וְעַל שְׁאָר הַתְּפִלָּה.** שֶׁיְּהוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל נוֹשָׁעִין מֵאוֹיְבֵיהֶם. וְחוֹתֵם בָּהּ עַמְּךָ יִשְׂרָאֵל צְרִיכִים יְשׁוּעָה בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה ה'‏ שׁוֹמֵעַ תְּפִלָּה:



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:8:1)


**בַּשְּׁמִינִי.** בְּשָׁנָה שְׁמִינִית שֶׁל שְׁמִיטָה שֶׁעָבְרָה, שֶׁהִיא מוֹצָאֵי שְׁבִיעִית זוֹ. בְּמוֹצָאֵי יוֹם טוֹב רִאשׁוֹן, דִּכְתִיב (דברים לא) בְּחַג הַסֻּכּוֹת, שׁוֹמֵעַ אֲנִי אֲפִלּוּ בְּיוֹם טוֹב אַחֲרוֹן שֶׁל חָג, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר שָׁם בְּבֹא כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל לֵרָאוֹת, מִתְּחִלַּת חֹל הַמּוֹעֵד:


[בשמיני] – In the eight year of the Sabbatical year–Shemittah that just passed, which is at the conclusion of this seventh year at the conclusion of the first day of Yom Tov (i.e., Sukkot), as it is written (Deuteronomy 31:10): “On the Feast of Booths.” I hear–learn even on the last day of Yom Tov of the holiday, the inference teaches us (Deuteronomy 31:11): “When all Israel comes to appear [before the LORD your God],”from the beginning of the Intermediate Days [of the Festival].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:8:2)


**אַגְרִיפָּס הַמֶּלֶךְ.** מִזַּרְעוֹ שֶׁל הוֹרוֹדוּס הָיָה, וּבְיָמָיו נֶחְרַב בַּיִת שֵׁנִי:


אגריפס המלך – he was from the seed of Herod and in his days, the Second Temple was destroyed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:8:3)


**זָלְגוּ עֵינָיו דְּמָעוֹת.** שֶׁהַמִּקְרָא הַזֶּה פְּסָלוֹ מִן הַמַּלְכוּת:


זלגו עיניו דמעות – fo this Biblical verse disqualifies him from being King.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:8:4)


**אָחִינוּ אָתָּה.** שֶׁאִמּוֹ מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל:


אחינו אתה – his mother is from the Israelites.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 7:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/7:8:5)


**וְקוֹרֵא מִתְּחִלַּת אֵלֶּה הַדְּבָרִים עַד שְׁמַע.** שֶׁבְּוָאֶתְחַנַּן. וּמְחַבֵּר עִם פָּרָשַׁת שְׁמַע פָּרָשַׁת וְהָיָה אִם שָׁמֹעַ, שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּפָרָשַׁת שְׁמַע קַבָּלַת מַלְכוּת שָׁמַיִם וּבְפָרָשַׁת וְהָיָה אִם שָׁמֹעַ קַבָּלַת עֹל מִצְוֹת. וּמְדַלֵּג, וְקוֹרֵא עַשֵּׂר תְּעַשֵּׂר. וּמִשָּׁם מְדַלֵּג וְקוֹרֵא כִּי תְכַלֶּה לַעְשֵׂר, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא זְמַן אָסִיף וּמַתְּנוֹת עֲנִיִּים וְהַפְרָשַׁת תְּרוּמוֹת וּמַעַשְׂרוֹת. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁפָּרָשַׁת הַמֶּלֶךְ בֵּין עַשֵּׂר לְכִי תְכַלֶּה לַעְשֵׂר הָיָה קוֹרֵא אוֹתָם יַחַד, שֶׁלֹּא לְהַפְסִיק בְּמַעַשְׂרוֹת. וְאַחַר כָּךְ בְּרָכוֹת וּקְלָלוֹת, שֶׁהֵן קַבָּלַת גְּזֵרוֹת וָעֳנָשִׁים שֶׁל תּוֹרָה. וְחוֹזֵר וְקוֹרֵא פָּרָשַׁת הַמֶּלֶךְ, שׂוֹם תָּשִׂים עָלֶיךָ מֶלֶךְ:


וקורא מתחלת אלה הדברים עד שמע – that is in [the Torah portion of] Vaethanan, and connects with the portion of the Shema (Deuteronomy 6:4-9) and “If you will surely hearken” (Deuteronomy 11:13-21), for in the portion of the Shema is the acceptance of the kingdom of heaven and in the “If you will surely hearken” portion is the acceptance of the yoke of the commandments and he skips and reads (Deuteronomy 14:22): You shall set aside every year a tenth part” and from there he skips and reads (Deuteronomy 26:1ven 2): “When you have set aside in full the tenth part of your yield,” because it is the time of the ingathering [of the crops] and the giving of gifts to the poor and separating the priest’s due and the tithes and even though the portion of the king is between the “You shall set aside every year a tenth part” and “When you have set aside in full the tenth part of your yield,” he reads them together to not interrupt the [verses about the] tithes and afterwards the blessings and curses (Deuteronomy chapter 27-28) which are the acceptance of the decrees and punishments of the Torah and then he returns and reads the portion of the king (Deuteronomy 17:15-20): “You shall be free to set a king over yourselves.”



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:1:1)


**מְשׁוּחַ מִלְחָמָה.** וְדִבֶּר אֶל הָעָם בִּלְשׁוֹן הַקֹּדֶשׁ. נֶאֱמַר כָּאן (דברים כ) וְדִבֵּר אֶל הָעָם, וְנֶאֱמַר לְהַלָּן (שמות יט) מֹשֶׁה יְדַבֵּר, מַה לְּהַלָּן בִּלְשׁוֹן הַקֹּדֶשׁ, אַף כָּאן בִּלְשׁוֹן הַקֹּדֶשׁ:


משוח מלחמה. ודבר אל העם בלשון הקדש – it is stated here (Deuteronomy 20:2): “[Before you join battle, the priest shall come forward] and address the troops.” And it is stated elsewhere (Exodus 19:19): “As Moses spoke, [God answered him in thunder].” Just as elsewhere is in the Holy Tongue (i.e., Hebrew), even here too is in the Holy Tongue.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:1:2)


**לְכָל כּוֹשֵׁל.** כָּל הַכּוֹשְׁלִים שֶׁאֵינָן יְכוֹלִין לָלֶכֶת בְּרַגְלֵיהֶן:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:1:3)


**הֲגָפַת תְּרִיסִין.** הַכָּאַת הַמָּגִנִּים זוֹ בְּזוֹ לְהַשְׁמִיעַ קוֹל וּלְאַיֵּם:


הגפת תריסין – striking of those defending each other to cause the hearing of a voice and to threaten.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:1:4)


**הַקַּלְגַּסִּין.** מַקְלוֹת שֶׁרָאשֵׁיהֶן עֲקוּמִים וּמַכִּין בָּהֶם עַל גַּבֵּי אֲבָנִים וְהֵם מְנַתְּזִים עַל הָאוֹיְבִים:


הקלגסין – sticks whose heads are curved and they beat them on stones and they fly off on their enemies.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:1:5)


**שׁוֹבַךְ.** שַׂר צָבָא הֲדַדְעֶזֶר בִּשְׁמוּאֵל (ב י):


שובך – the officer of the army Hadarezer in [Second] Samuel [10:16].



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:2:1)


**אֶחָד הַבּוֹנֶה וְאֶחָד הַלּוֹקֵחַ.** בַּיִת בָּנוּי, שֶׁהֲרֵי אֶצְלוֹ הוּא חָדָשׁ:


אחד הבונה ואחד הלוקח – a house built which for him was new.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:2:2)


**וְאֶחָד הַנּוֹטֵעַ חֲמִשָּׁה אִילָנֵי מַאֲכָל.** דִּבְהָכִי הָוֵי כֶּרֶם, שְׁתַּיִם כְּנֶגֶד שְׁתַּיִם, וְאַחַת יוֹצֵאת זָנָב:


ואחד הנוטע חמשה אילני מאכל – and in this, it is a vineyard, two against two, and one goes out with a tail, and even from five types they combine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:2:3)


**וַאֲפִלּוּ מֵחֲמֵשֶׁת מִינִים.** מִצְטָרְפִים:



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:3:1)


**אִילָנֵי סְרָק.** כְּגוֹן אֲרָזִים וְשִׁקְמִים שֶׁאֵינָם עוֹשִׂים פְּרִי:


אילני סרק – such as cedar and sycamore that don’t produce fruit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:3:2)


**וְהַמַּחֲזִיר אֶת גְּרוּשָׁתוֹ.** וְאֵרְסָהּ, אֵינוֹ חוֹזֵר, שֶׁאֵינָהּ חֲדָשָׁה לוֹ, וּקְרָא כְּתִיב אִשָּׁה חֲדָשָׁה:


והמחזיר את גרושתו – and he betrothed her, he does not return to her for she is not new to him and in Scripture it is written (Deuteronomy 24:5): “[When a man has taken] a new bride, [he shall not go out with the army or be assigned to it for any purpose].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:3:3)


**אַלְמָנָה.** וְאֵרְסָהּ כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל, אֵינוֹ חוֹזֵר בִּשְׁבִילָהּ, דִּכְתִיב וְלֹא לְקָחָהּ, וְהָיָה אֶפְשָׁר לוֹמַר וְלֹא לָקַח, מַאי לְקָחָהּ, שֶׁתְּהֵא רְאוּיָה לוֹ לִלְקִיחָה:


אלמנה – and a High Priest betrothed her, he does not return [to her – a widow],as it is written (Deuteronomy 20:7): “[Is there anyone who has paid the bride-price for a wife,] but has not yet married er? [Let him go back to his home, lest he die in battle and another marry her],” but it was possible. To state, and he did not take her? What is “to take her?” that she is appropriate for him to take her [as a wife].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:3:4)


**עַל מְכוֹנוֹ.** סְתָרוֹ וּבְנָאוֹ כְּמִדָּה רִאשׁוֹנָה. אֵינוֹ חוֹזֵר, דְּלָאו חָדָשׁ הוּא, וְגָרַע מֵהַלּוֹקֵחַ וְהַיּוֹרֵשׁ:


על מכונו – tore it down and built it like the first measure, he does not return [to live in it], for it is not new and it is worse than someone who purchases it or who inherits it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:3:5)


**הַבּוֹנֶה בֵית לְבֵנִים בַּשָּׁרוֹן.** מָקוֹם הוּא שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ שָׁרוֹן, וְאֵין הַקַּרְקַע שֶׁלּוֹ יָפָה לִלְבֵנִים, וְלֹא הָוֵי דָּבָר שֶׁל קְיָמָא, שֶׁהָיוּ צְרִיכִין לְחַדְּשָׁהּ שְׁנֵי פְּעָמִים כָּל שֶׁבַע שָׁנִים:


הבונה בית לבנים בשרון – it is a place whose name is Sharon, and its land is not nice for bricks and it is not something enduring for they had to renew it twice every seven years.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:4:1)


**וְאֵלּוּ שֶׁאֵין זָזִין מִמְּקוֹמָן.** דְּהָנָךְ דִּלְעֵיל הוֹלְכִים עַד הַגְּבוּל וְשָׁם שׁוֹמְעִין דִּבְרֵי כֹהֵן, וְחוֹזְרִים מִן הַמַּעֲרָכָה לְתוֹךְ אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְאֵין חוֹזְרִין לְבָתֵּיהֶן אֶלָּא עוֹסְקִים לְסַפֵּק מַיִם וּמָזוֹן לַהוֹלְכִים לַמִּלְחָמָה. וְאֵלּוּ אֵין זָזִין מִמְּקוֹמָן וַאֲפִלּוּ לָלֶכֶת עַד הַסְּפָר וְלָשׁוּב עַל פִּי כֹּהֵן:


ואלו שאין זזין ממקומן – for those above go until the border. And there, we hear the words of the Kohen and they return from the battlefield into the midst of the land of Israel. But they do not return to their homes but rather engage in providing water and food to those who go to war and these do not move from their places and even to go to the border line and to return at the direction of the Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:4:2)


**הַבּוֹנֶה בַיִת וַחֲנָכוֹ.** וְלֹא דָּר בּוֹ עֲדַיִן שָׁנָה. וְכֵן הַנּוֹטֵעַ כֶּרֶם וְחִלְּלוֹ, וַעֲדַיִן לֹא עָבְרָה שָׁנָה רְבִיעִית שְׁלֵמָה עָלָיו:


הבונה בית וחנכו – and do not live in it for a year yet, and similarly, one who plants a vineyard and redeem it, and the fourth year has not yet passed upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:4:3)


**נָקִי יִהְיֶה לְבֵיתוֹ.** בִּשְׁבִיל בֵּיתוֹ:


נקי יהיה לביתו – for his house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:4:4)


**יִהְיֶה.** לְרִבּוּיָא הוּא. הוֹסִיף לְךָ הַכָּתוּב הֲוָיָה אַחֶרֶת לִהְיוֹת כָּזוֹ:


יהיה – an amplification which the Biblical verse adds to you which is something other that should be like this.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:5:1)


**וְיָסְפוּ הַשֹּׁטְרִים.** עַל דִּבְרֵי כֹּהֵן:


ויספו השוטרים – on the words of the Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:5:2)


**בְּקִשְׁרֵי הַמִּלְחָמָה.** כְּשֶׁמִתְקַשְּׁרִין לַעֲמֹד צְפוּפִין שֶׁלֹּא יְפָרְקוּם אֹיְבִים:


בקשרי המלחמה – that they become knotty to stand squeezed together that the enemies will not separate them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:5:3)


**לְפִיכָךְ.** שֶׁהַיָּרֵא זֶהוּ הַיָּרֵא מֵעֲבֵרוֹת שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ, תָּלְתָה תּוֹרָה בְּבָנָה וְנָטַע וְאֵרֵס לַחְזֹר בִּגְלָלָן, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְלוּ בְּכָךְ הַחֲזָרָה וְלֹא יִתְבַּיֵּשׁ לוֹמַר עָלָיו מֵעֲבֵרוֹת שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ הוּא יָרֵא וְחוֹזֵר:


לפיכך – the person who is fearful is the person who fears from sins that are in his hand, the Torah hung “built, and planted and betrothed” to return on their account, so that the return would be dependent upon this and he would not be embarrassed to state regarding it from the sins that are in his hand and he is fearful and returns.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:5:4)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר אַלְמָנָה לְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל וְכוּ'.** לְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי הַגְּלִילִי אֲפִלּוּ עָבַר עַל דִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים חוֹזֵר. וּלְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי עַד שֶׁיַּעֲבֹר עַל דִּבְרֵי תּוֹרָה, דּוּמְיָא דְּאַלְמָנָה לְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל:


ר' יוסי אומר אלמנה לכהן גדול וכו' – for Rabbi Yosi HaGlili, even if he transgressed on the words of the Scribes, he returns, for according to Rabbi Yosi, until he transgresses the words of the Torah is similar to a widow marrying a High Priest.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:6:1)


**וּבַעֲקֵבוֹ שֶׁל עָם.** בְּסוֹפוֹ שֶׁל עָם, שֶׁמְּשַׁמְּרִים שֶׁלֹּא יַחְזְרוּ הָאַחֲרוֹנִים לַאֲחוֹרֵיהֶם לָנוּס:


ובעקבו של עם – at the rear of the people who guard that the last ones who are behind them should not flee.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:6:2)


**זְקִיפִים.** גִּבּוֹרִים מְמֻנִּים לְכָךְ שֶׁאִם יִפֹּל אֶחָד מִן הַנִּלְחָמִים יַעֲמִידוּהוּ וְיִזְקְפוּהוּ:


זקפים – warriors appointed to this, for if one of the fighters falls, he will restore him and raise him up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:6:3)


**וַאֲחֵרִים מֵאֲחוֹרֵיהֶם וְכַשִּׁילִין שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל בִּידֵיהֶם.** לִשְׁמֹר שֶׁלֹּא יָנוּסוּ:


ואחרים מאחוריהם וכשילין של ברזל – to guard that they will not flee.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:6:4)


**כַּשִּׁילִין.** קַרְדוֹמוֹת שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל:


כשילים – iron hatchets–spades.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:6:5)


**לְקַפֵּחַ אֶת שׁוֹקָיו.** לַחֲתֹּךְ אֶת שׁוֹקָיו:


לקפח את שוקיו – to sever his foreleg.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:6:6)


**שֶׁתְּחִלַּת נִיסָה נְפִילָה.** לָשׁוֹן מְסֹרָס הוּא, שֶׁתְּחִלַּת נְפִילָה נִיסָּה:


שתחלת ניסה נפילה – it is transposed language for the beginning of falling (i.e., in slaughter) is the rout (see Talmud Sotah 44b).



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:7:1)


**בַּמֶּה דְבָרִים אֲמוּרִים.** שֶׁחוֹזְרִים מִן הַמַּעֲרָכָה, וְיֵשׁ שֶׁאֵין זָזִין מִמְּקוֹמָם. בְּמִלְחֶמֶת הָרְשׁוּת:


בד"א – that they return from the battlefield and there are those who do not move from their places in an optional war.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/8:7:2)


**אֲבָל מִלְחֶמֶת מִצְוָה הַכֹּל יוֹצְאִים.** מִלְחֶמֶת אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל וּמִלְחֶמֶת עֲמָלֵק הַכֹּל מוֹדִים שֶׁהִיא חוֹבָה וְהַכֹּל יוֹצְאִים בָּהּ. וּמִלְחֶמֶת בֵּית דָּוִד שֶׁהִיא לִרְוָחָה לְהַרְחִיב גְּבוּל יִשְׂרָאֵל וְלָקַחַת מַס מֵהָעַמִּים כְּגוֹן מִלְחֶמֶת אֲרַם נַהֲרַיִם וַאֲרַם צוֹבָה, הַכֹּל מוֹדִים שֶׁהִיא רְשׁוּת. לֹא נֶחְלְקוּ תַּנָּא קַמָּא וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה אֶלָּא בְּמִלְחָמָה שֶׁנִּלְחָמִים יִשְׂרָאֵל עִם אוֹיְבֵיהֶם כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִתְגַּבְּרוּ עֲלֵיהֶם וְיָצֵרוּ לָהֶם. תַּנָּא קַמָּא קָרֵי לָהּ מִלְחֶמֶת רְשׁוּת וְסוֹבֵר שֶׁאֵין אָדָם רַשַּׁאי לְהִתְבַּטֵּל מִן הַמִּצְוָה כְּדֵי לְהִלָּחֵם בְּמִלְחָמָה כָּזוֹ. וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה סָבַר שֶׁגַּם זוֹ מִלְחֶמֶת מִצְוָה, מֵאַחַר שֶׁהוּא לְהוֹשִׁיעַ אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל מִיַּד שׂוֹנְאֵיהֶם, וְהָעוֹסֵק בְּמִצְוָה פָּטוּר מִן הַמִּצְוָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אבל מלחמת מצוה הכל יוצאים – a war of the Land of Israel, and the war against Amalek. Everyone admits that it is obligatory, and everyone goes out in it [to fight]. But the wars of the House of David to extend and widen the brder of Israel and to assess taxes from peoples such as the war of Aram Naharayim and Aram Tzobah (Syria), everyone agrees that it is option. But the First Tanna–Teacher and Rabbi Yehudah did not disagree other than with a war that Israel fights with its enemies in order that they not grow stronger over them and trouble hem. The first Tanna–teacher calls that an optional war, and holds that a person is not permitted to make himself idle from the commandment in order to fight in a war like this, but Rabbi Yehuda holds that this also is a commanded war, for after it is to save Israel from the hand of their enemies, and one who engages in a commandment is exempt from another commandment. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:1:1)


**עֶגְלָה עֲרוּפָה בִּלְשׁוֹן הַקֹּדֶשׁ.** מַה שֶּׁהַזְּקֵנִים אוֹמְרִים יָדֵינוּ לֹא שָׁפְכוּ אֶת הַדָּם הַזֶּה וְהַכֹּהֲנִים אוֹמְרִים כַּפֵּר לְעַמְּךָ יִשְׂרָאֵל וְגוֹ', בִּלְשׁוֹן הַקֹּדֶשׁ הֻזְקְקוּ לְאָמְרָן:


עגלה ערופה בלשון הקדש – what the elders say (Deuteronomy 21:7): “Our hands did not shed this blood,” and the Kohanim state (Deuteronomy 27:8): “Absolve, O LORD, Your people Israel [whom You redeemed, and do not let guilt for the blood of the innocent remain among Your people Israel],” it was necessary to state this in the Holy Tongue (i.e., Hebrew).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:1:2)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר כִּי יִמָּצֵא כוּ'.** וּבְאוֹתוֹ פָּרָשָׁה נֶאֱמָר וְעָנוּ וְאָמְרוּ, וְנֶאֱמַר לְהַלָּן (דברים כז) וְעָנוּ הַלְוִיִּם וְאָמְרוּ, מַה לְּהַלָּן בִּלְשׁוֹן הַקֹּדֶשׁ, אַף כָּאן בִּלְשׁוֹן הַקֹּדֶשׁ:


שנאמר כי ימצא וכו' – and in the same portion it states (Deuteronomy 21:7): ‘And they shall make this declaration,” and it states further on (Deuteronomy 27:14): “The Levites shall then proclaim [in a loud voice to all the people of Israel.” Just as there it is in the Holy Tongue (i.e., Hebrew), even here is in the Holy Tongue.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:1:3)


**הָיוּ יוֹצְאִים.** וּבָאִים שָׁם וּמוֹדְדִים מִן הֶחָלָל לְצַד הָעֲיָרוֹת שֶׁסְּבִיבוֹתָיו לֵידַע אֵיזוֹ קְרוֹבָה:


היו יוצאים – and they come there and measure from the slain to the side of the villages that surround him to know which is closest.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:1:4)
