**אֵין בֵּית דִּין שָׁקוּל.** אֵין בֵּית דִּין עֲשׂוּיִין שֶׁיְּהֵא אֶפְשָׁר לַחְלֹק שָׁוֶה בְּשָׁוֶה, אֶלָּא שֶׁיְּהֵא הָרֹב לְצַד אֶחָד לְקַיֵּם אַחֲרֵי רַבִּים לְהַטּוֹת. (שמות כג):


אין ב"ד שקול – a Jewish court is not made where it will be possible to divide into equal shares but rather, that the majority will be on the one side, to fulfill (Exodus 23:2): “[you shall not give perverse testimony in a dispute] so as to pervert in favor of the mighty.”



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:2:1)


**נִמְצָא סָמוּךְ לַסְּפָר.** אֵצֶל גְּבוּל הַגּוֹיִם:


נמצא סמוך לספר – near the border of non-Jews


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:2:2)


**לֹא הָיוּ מוֹדְדִים.** דִּכְתִיב כִּי יִמָּצֵא חָלָל, פְּרָט לְמַמְצִיא אֶת עַצְמוֹ, שֶׁסָּמוּךְ לִגְבוּל הַגּוֹיִם כְּאִלּוּ הִמְצִיא עַצְמוֹ לְמִיתָה הוּא:


לא היו מודדים – they would not measure, as it is written (Deuteronomy 21:1): “someone slain is found [lying in the open],” excluding someone who brings himself that is nearby the border of the non-Jews as if he brought himself to death.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:2:3)


**לְעִיר שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ בֵּית דִּין לֹא הָיוּ מוֹדְדִין.** אֵין מוֹדְדִין אֶלָּא מֵעִיר שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ בֵּית דִּין. הָכִי קָאָמַר, נִמְצָא סָמוּךְ לְעִיר שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ בֵּית דִּין, אֵין מוֹדְדִין לָהּ, אֶלָּא לְעִיר שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ בֵּית דִּין מוֹדְדִין, אֵיזוֹ עִיר שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ בֵּית דִּין קְרוֹבָה לוֹ. וְאִי לֹא הַדַר תַּנְיֵהּ, הֲוָה אֲמִינָא אֵין מוֹדְדִין כְּלָל, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר וְלָקְחוּ זִקְנֵי הָעִיר, מִכָּל מָקוֹם, דְּהַאי הָעִיר קְרָא יְתֵרָא הוּא, דְּמָצֵי לְמִכְתָּב וְהָיָה הָעִיר הַקְּרוֹבָה אֶל הֶחָלָל וְיָצְאוּ זְקֵנֶיהָ:


לעיר שאין בה ב"ד לא היו מודדין. אין מודדין אלא מעיר שיש בה ב"ד – this is what he said: if he is found near a city that lacks in it a Jewish court, we don’t measure it, but rather, [near to] a city that has a Jewish court close to it. What city that has a Jewish court near it? And if he did not repeat this teaching, I would think that we don’t measure at all, the inference teaches us (Deuteronomy 21:3): “The elders of the town [nearest the corpse] shall then take a [heifer which has never been worked, which has never pulled in a yoke],” anyמway, for this “the city” is an additional Biblical verse” for one could have written “and the city that is closest to the slain and its elders went out.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:2:4)


**מְכֻוָּן.** מְצֻמְצָם, שֶׁאֵין זוֹ קְרוֹבָה לוֹ מִזּוֹ אֲפִלּוּ מְלֹא חוּט:


מכוון – squeezed in–confined, that it is not closer to this or fro that one, even as much as a thread’s length.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:2:5)


**שְׁתֵּיהֶן מְבִיאוֹת שְׁתֵּי עֲגָלוֹת דִּבְרֵי רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר.** וְאֵין כֵּן הֲלָכָה, אֶלָּא שְׁתֵּיהֶן מְבִיאוֹת עֶגְלָה אַחַת בְּשֻׁתְּפוּת. וְהָכִי אָמְרִינַן בַּגְּמָרָא בִּבְכוֹרוֹת:


מביאות שתי עגלות דברי ר"א – but the Halakha is not like this, but rather, both bring one heifer jointly . And we state this in the Gemara in [Tractate] Bekhorot [18a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:2:6)


**אֵין יְרוּשָׁלַיִם מְבִיאָה עֶגְלָה עֲרוּפָה.** דִּכְתִיב (דברים כא) בָּאֲדָמָה אֲשֶׁר ה'‏ אֱלֹהֶיךָ נוֹתֵן לְךָ נַחֲלָה לְרִשְׁתָּהּ, פְּרָט לִירוּשָׁלַיִם שֶׁלֹּא נִתְחַלְּקָה לִשְׁבָטִים:


אין ירושלם מביאה עגלה ערופה – as it is written (Deuteronomy 21:1): “in the land that the LORD your God is assigning you [as a possession) to possess,” excluding Jerusalem that was not allotted to the tribes.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:3:1)


**נִמְצָא רֹאשׁוֹ בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד.** הַשְׁתָּא לֹא מַיְרֵי תַּנָּא לְעִנְיַן מְדִידָה. דְּמִדְּקָתָנֵי סֵיפָא מֵאַיִן הָיוּ מוֹדְדִין, מִכְּלָל דְּרֵישָׁא לָאו בַּמְּדִידָה עָסְקִינַן. אֶלָּא בְּמֵת מִצְוָה קָנָה מְקוֹמוֹ עָסְקִינַן, דְּהֵיכָא דְנִמְצָא רֹאשׁוֹ בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד וְגוּפוֹ בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר, מוֹלִיכִין רֹאשׁוֹ אֵצֶל גּוּפוֹ וְקוֹבְרִין אוֹתוֹ בַּמָּקוֹם שֶׁנִּמְצָא הַגּוּף, דְּגוּפֵיהּ בְּדוּכְתֵּיהּ נָפַל וְרֵישָׁא הוּא דְּנָיֵיד, דִּבְרֵי רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


נמצא ראשו במקום אחד – now the Tanna is not speaking about the matter of measuring, since the end (actually, the beginning of Mishnah 4 of this chapter) teaches, from where do they measure–"מאין מודדין" – it follows that in the first part of the Mishnah we do not deal with measurement, but rather, we are dealing with the corpse of a person whose relatives are unknown and whose burial is obligatory on everyone that acquires its place for where his head is found in one place and his body in another place, we bring the head to the body and we bury it where the body is found, for the body fell in its place for the head is unsteady, which are the words of Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:3:2)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר מוֹלִיכִין הַגּוּף אֵצֶל הָרֹאשׁ.** דְּרֵישָׁא הֵיכָא דְּנָפֵיל נָפֵיל וְגוּפֵיהּ רָהֵיט וְאָזֵיל. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


רבי עקיבא אומר מוליכין הגוף אצל הראש – for the head, where it fell, it fell, and its body runs and goes, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:4:1)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר מִטִּבּוּרוֹ.** קָסָבַר עִקַּר חַיּוּתָא בְטִבּוּרֵיהּ הוּא:


ר:א אומר מטיבורו – he holds that the real life is in his navel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:4:2)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר מֵחָטְמוֹ.** עִקַּר חַיּוּתָא מֵחָטְמוֹ הוּא, דִּכְתִיב (בראשית ז) כָּל אֲשֶׁר נִשְׁמַת רוּחַ חַיִּים בְּאַפָּיו:


ר' עקיבא אומר מחוטמו – real life is from his nose (cessation of breath from the nostrils is the main sign of death – see Talmud Yoma 85a), as it is written (Genesis 7:22): “All in whose nostrils was the merest breath of life.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:4:3)


**מִמְּקוֹם שֶׁנַּעֲשֶׂה חָלָל מִצַּוָּארוֹ.** דְּאֵין קָרוּי חָלָל אֶלָּא מִן הַצַּוָּאר, כְּדִכְתִיב (יחזקאל כא) עַל צַוְּארֵי חַלְלֵי רְשָׁעִים, הִלְכָּךְ מִמֶּנּוּ הָיוּ מוֹדְדִים, כְּדִכְתִיב אֶל הֶחָלָל. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


מקום שנעשה חלל מצוארו – it is not called slain, other than from the neck, as it is written (Ezekiel 21:34): “you shall be wielded over the necks of the dishonored withed ones.” Therefore, from it (i.e., the neck) they would measure, as it is written (Deuteronomy 21:3): “[The elders of the town nearest] to the corpse,” But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:5:1)


**נִפְטְרוּ זִקְנֵי יְרוּשָׁלַיִם.** שֶׁאֵינָן בָּאִין אֶלָּא לִמְדֹּד, כְּדִכְתִיב וְיָצְאוּ זְקֵנֶיךָ וְשֹׁפְטֶיךָ וּמָדְדוּ:


נפטרו זקני ירושלם – who do not come other than to measure, as it is written (Deuteronomy 21:2): “Your elders and magistrates shall go out and measure [the distances from the corpse to the nearby towns].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:5:2)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ אֵיתָן כָּשֵׁר.** שֶׁלֹּא נֶאֱמַר אֵיתָן לְעִכּוּבָא אֶלָּא לְמִצְוָה:


אע"פ שאינו איתן כשר – it did not state "איתן"–everflowing–strong wadi (Deuteronomy 21:4) as an indispensable condition but rather for the Mitzvah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:5:3)


**בְּקוֹפִיץ.** סַכִּין גָּדוֹל:


בקופיץ – a large knife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:5:4)


**מֵאֲחוֹרֶיהָ.** מִמּוּל עָרְפָּהּ:


מאחריה – opposite its neck.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:5:5)


**מְקוֹמָהּ אָסוּר.** לְעוֹלָם:


מקומה אסור – forever.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:5:6)


**וּמֻתָּר לִסְרֹק שָׁם פִּשְׁתָּן.** עֲבוֹדָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ בְּגוּפָהּ שֶׁל קַרְקַע, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כא) אֲשֶׁר לֹא יֵעָבֵד וְלֹא יִזָּרֵעַ, מַה זְּרִיעָה מְיֻחֶדֶת שֶׁהִיא בְּגוּפוֹ שֶׁל קַרְקַע, אַף כָּל עֲבוֹדָה שֶׁהִיא בְּגוּפוֹ שֶׁל קַרְקַע:


ומותר לסרוק שם פשתן – work that is not on the body of the land, as it is written (Deuteronomy 21:4): “[a heifer] which has never been worked, which has never pulled a yoke.” Just as sowing is special which is on the main body of the land, so also, all work which is on the main body of the land.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:5:7)


**לְנַקֵּר שָׁם אֲבָנִים.** לְסַתֵּת שָׁם אֲבָנִים:


לנקר את האבנים – to chisel the stones.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:6:1)


**לֹא בָא לְיָדֵינוּ וּפְטַרְנוּהוּ.** בְּלֹא מְזוֹנוֹת:


ולא בא לידינו ופטרנוהו – without food.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:6:2)


**וְלֹא רְאִינוּהוּ וְהִנַּחְנוּהוּ.** בְּלֹא לְוָיָה:


ולא ראענוהו והנחנוהו – without accompaniment.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:7:1)


**תֵּצֵא וְתִרְעֶה בָעֵדֶר.** כִּשְׁאָר חֻלִּין:


תצא ותרעה בעדר – like other non-holy animals.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:7:2)


**כִּפְּרָה סְפֵקָהּ.** הִיא עָשְׂתָה אֶת שֶׁלָּהּ:


כפרה ספיקה – which had performed its own [work].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:7:3)


**וְאַחַר כָּךְ נִמְצָא הַהוֹרֵג הֲרֵי זֶה יֵהָרֵג.** דִּכְתִיב בְּסוֹף פָּרָשַׁת עֶגְלָה עֲרוּפָה (דברים כא) וְאַתָּה תְּבַעֵר הַדָּם הַנָּקִי מִקִּרְבֶּךָ:


ואחר כך נמצא ההורגד ה"ז יהרג – as it is written at the end of the portion of the heifer whose neck was broken (Deuteronomy 21:9): “Thus you will remove from your midst guilt for the blood of the innocent.”



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:8:1)


**עֵד אֶחָד אוֹמֵר רָאִיתִי וְעֵד אֶחָד אוֹמֵר לֹא רָאִיתָ.** וּכְגוֹן שֶׁבָּאוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם בְּבַת אַחַת, [אָז] הָיוּ עוֹרְפִים. אֲבָל אִם בָּא אֶחָד תְּחִלָּה וְאָמַר רָאִיתִי אֶת הַהוֹרֵג, וְהֵימְנוּהוּ כְּבֵי תְּרֵי, שֶׁהַתּוֹרָה הֶאֱמִינַתְהוּ דִּכְתִיב (שם) לֹא נוֹדַע מִי הִכָּהוּ, הָא נוֹדַע אֲפִלּוּ עַל פִּי עֵד אֶחָד אֵין עוֹרְפִין, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבָּא עֵד אֶחָד אַחַר כָּךְ וְהִכְחִישׁוֹ, אֵינוֹ נֶאֱמָן הַשֵּׁנִי, שֶׁאֵין דְּבָרָיו שֶׁל אֶחָד בִּמְקוֹם שְׁנַיִם:


עד אחד אומר ראיתי ועד אחד אומר לא ראית – as for example when both came at the same time [then] they would break the neck. But if one came first and said “I saw the killer”, and they believed him like two [witnesses]. For the Torah believed him, as it is written (Deuteronomy 21:1): “the identity of the slayer not being known,” but if they did know, even through one witness, they don’t break the neck, even though one witness had come and afterwards his testimony is contradicted, the second is not believed, because the words of the one is not in the of two [witnesses].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:8:2)


**עֵד אֶחָד אוֹמֵר רָאִיתִי וּשְׁנַיִם אוֹמְרִים לֹא רָאִיתָ הָיוּ עוֹרְפִים.** וּכְגוֹן שֶׁהַשְּׁנַיִם וְהָאֶחָד פְּסוּלֵי עֵדוּת הֵן, דְּבִפְסוּלֵי עֵדוּת הֲלֵךְ אַחַר רֹב דֵּעוֹת:


עד אחד אומר ראיתי ושנים אומרים לא ראית היו עורפים – as, for example, the two [witnesses] and the one are invalid witnesses, for in the case of invalid witnesses, one goes according to the majority of opinions.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:9:1)


**בָּטְלָה עֶגְלָה עֲרוּפָה.** לְפִי שֶׁמַּכִּירִין הָיוּ בָּהֶם מִי רָגִיל לַהֲרֹג:


בטלה עגלה ערופה – because they knew of them who was accustomed to kill.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:9:2)


**בָּטְלוּ הָאֶשְׁכּוֹלוֹת.** אִישׁ שֶׁהַכֹּל בּוֹ. כְּלוֹמַר תּוֹרָתָם אֱמֶת מִבְּלִי דֹפִי:


בטלו האשכולות – a person who has everything (this is a Notarikon – describing the word אשכולות – see the commentary of Tosafot Yom Tov who also quotes Maimonides on this matter), meaning to say, their Torah is true without reproach.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:10:1)


**הוֹדָיַת מַעֲשֵׂר.** וִדּוּי מַעֲשֵׂר, בִּעַרְתִּי הַקֹּדֶשׁ מִן הַבַּיִת וְגַם נְתַתִּיו לַלֵוִי. לְפִי שֶׁעֶזְרָא קָנַס אֶת הַלְוִיִּם שֶׁלֹּא יִתְּנוּ לָהֶם מַעֲשֵׂר כְּשֶׁעָלָה מִן הַגּוֹלָה וּבְנֵי לֵוִי לֹא עָלוּ עִמּוֹ, וְצִוָּה שֶׁיִּתְּנוּ הַמַּעֲשֵׂר לַכֹּהֲנִים. וְיוֹחָנָן כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל בִּטֵּל הַוִּדּוּי, שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה יָכוֹל לוֹמַר וְגַם נְתַתִּיו לַלֵּוִי:


הודיית מעשר – the confession of the tithe (Deuteronomy 26:13): “I have cleared out the consecrated portion from the house; and I have given it to the Levite,” because Ezra fined the Levites that they did not give to them the tithe [of the tithe] when he came up [to the Land of Israel] from the Diaspora, and the Levites did not go up with him, and he (i.e., Ezra) commanded that they should give the tithe [of the tithe] to the Kohanim, and Yohanan the High Priest annulled the confession because he was not able to say, “and I have given it to the Levite.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:10:2)


**בִּטֵּל אֶת הַמְעוֹרְרִים.** שֶׁהָיוּ הַלְוִיִּם בְּכָל יוֹם אוֹמְרִים עַל הַדּוּכָן עוּרָה לָמָּה תִישַׁן ה', אָמַר וְכִי יֵשׁ שֵׁינָה לִפְנֵי הַמָּקוֹם, וְעָמַד וּבִטְּלָן:


בטל את המעוררים – for the Levites would say on each day on the platform (Psalms 44:24): “rouse yourself, why do you sleep, O God.” He stated: Is there sleep before God? (see Talmud Sotah 48a and Tosefta Sotah 13:9). And he stood and annulled them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:10:3)


**אֶת הַנּוֹקְפִין.** שֶׁהָיוּ מְסַרְטִין לָעֵגֶל שֶׁל קָרְבָּן בֵּין קַרְנָיו כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּפֹּל לוֹ דָּם בְּעֵינָיו כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִרְאֶה וִיהֵא נוֹחַ לְכָפְתוֹ וּלְשָׁחְטוֹ. וְעָמַד הוּא וּבִטְּלוֹ, שֶׁנִּרְאֶה כְּבַעַל מוּם. וְהִתְקִין לָהֶם טַבָּעוֹת בַּקַּרְקַע לְהַכְנִיס צַוַּאר הַבְּהֵמָה לְתוֹכָהּ:


ואת הנקפין – that they would wound the calf of the sacrifice between its horns in order that blood would fall between its eyes so that it would not see and it would be easy to bend over and to slaughter it and he would stand and annul it for it appeared as having a blemish (and unfit for the altar) and he established for them rings in the ground for them to place the neck of the animal within it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:10:4)


**נוֹקְפִין.** מַכִּין. וְדֻגְמָתוֹ [חֻלִּין ז:] אֵין אָדָם נוֹקֵף אֶצְבָּעוֹ מִלְּמַטָּה:


נוקפין – beat and its example [Tractate Hullin 7b], “no person bruises his finger here on earth [unless it was so decreed against him in heaven].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:10:5)


**הָיָה פַטִּישׁ מַכֶּה בִירוּשָׁלַיִם.** בְּחֹל הַמּוֹעֵד. חָרָשֵׁי נְחֹשֶׁת וּבַרְזֶל הָיוּ מַכִּין בַּפַּטִּישׁ לַעֲשׂוֹת מְלֶאכֶת הָאָבֵד שֶׁהִיא מֻתֶּרֶת בַּמּוֹעֵד. וְעָמַד הוּא וּבִטְּלָן, מִשּׁוּם דְּאַוְּשָׁא מִלְּתָא טוּבָא וְאִיכָּא זִלְזוּל מוֹעֵד:


היה פטיש מכה בירושלים – on the Intermediate days of the Festival those artisans of copper and iron would beat with a hammer in order to perform work which cannot be postponed without irretrievable loss, and he stood and cancelled them because the thing becomes more known and there is a disregard of the Intermediate Days of the Festival.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:10:6)


**וּבְיָמָיו אֵין אָדָם צָרִיךְ לִשְׁאֹל עַל הַדְּמָאי.** שֶׁהוּא אָמַר לִבְנֵי דוֹרוֹ כְּשֵׁם שֶׁתְּרוּמָה גְדוֹלָה עֲוֹן מִיתָה, כָּךְ תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר וְטֶבֶל עֲוֹן מִיתָה. וְתִקֵּן שֶׁיּוֹצִיאוּ מִן הַדְּמַאי תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר וּמַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי בִּלְבַד, וְלֹא יוֹצִיאוּ מִמֶּנּוּ מַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן וְלֹא מַעֲשַׂר עָנִי, שֶׁיְּכוֹלִין לוֹמַר לַלֵּוִי אוֹ לָעָנִי הָבֵא רְאָיָה שֶׁהוּא טֶבֶל וְטֹל. וּמִתַּקָּנָה זוֹ וָאֵילָךְ הַלּוֹקֵחַ פֵּרוֹת מִן הַשּׁוּק לֹא הָיָה שׁוֹאֵל אִם הֵן מְתֻקָּנִין אִם לָאו, אֶלָּא מִיָּד מַפְרִישׁ מֵהֶם תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר וּמַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי וְאוֹכֵל אֶת הַשְּׁאָר, שֶׁכָּל הַלּוֹקֵחַ מֵעַם הָאָרֶץ פֵּרוֹת הֵם בְּחֶזְקַת שֶׁהֵם דְּמַאי:


ובימיו אין אדם צריך לשאול על הדמאי – for he said to the people of his generation that just as regarding the great heave offering–priest’s share of the crop, its punishment [for non-fulfilment] is death, so too, the tenth of the tenth [given by the Levites to the Kohanim] and separation of priestly shares is required before one is able to partake of them, [non-fulfillment] is punishable by death and he established that they should take out from the doubtfully tithed produce only the tenth-of-a-tenth and the Second Tithe, and they should not take out it the First Tithe and the Poor Man’s Tithe for one can say to the Levite and–or the Poor Person: Bring proof that it is produce from which priestly gifts and tithes had not yet been separated and take it. And from this ordinance and onward, a person who takes his fruit from the marketplace would not ask if they had been properly prepared [by the giving of the priestly gifts and tithes] or not, but immediately, he separates from them the tenth-of-the-tenth and the Second Tithe, and consumes the rest for anyone who purchases from those who did not observe certain religious customs regarding tithing, their produce is considered doubtfully tithed.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:11:1)


**סַנְהֶדְרִין.** שֵׁם הַדַּיָּנִים. וְנִקְרָאִים סַנְהֶדְרִין שֶׁשּׂוֹנְאִים הַדְרַת פָּנִים בַּדִּין:


סנהדרין – the judges are there, and they are called a Sanhedrin in that they detest a majestic appearance–an imposing figure in judgment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:11:2)


**בָּטַל הַשִּׁיר.** דִּכְתִיב (איכה ה) זְקֵנִים מִשַּׁעַר שָׁבָתוּ בַּחוּרִים מִנְּגִינָתָם, בַּזְּמַן שֶׁבָּטְלוּ הַזְּקֵנִים הַיּוֹשְׁבִים בַּשַּׁעַר שֶׁהֵם הַסַּנְהֶדְרִין, בָּטְלוּ הַבַּחוּרִים מִנְּגִינָתָם גַּם כֵּן:


בטל השיר – as it is written (Lamentations 5:14): “The old men are gone from the gate, The young men from their music” when the elders who were the Sanhedrin who were sitting at the gate were made idle, the young men were also made idle from their music.



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:12:1)


**מִשֶּׁמֵּתוּ נְבִיאִים הָרִאשׁוֹנִים.** כָּל הַנְּבִיאִים נִקְרָאִים נְבִיאִים רִאשׁוֹנִים, חוּץ מֵחַגַּי זְכַרְיָה וּמַלְאָכִי וְחַבְרֵיהֶן דְּאַחֲרוֹנִים נִינְהוּ, שֶׁהָיוּ בְּבִנְיַן בַּיִת שֵׁנִי:


משמתו נביאים הראשונים – all the prophets are called the Early Prophets, except for Haggai, Zechariah and Malachi and their associates for they were the Latter [Prophets] who were during the days of the Second Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:12:2)


**בָּטַל הַשָּׁמִיר.** כְּמִין תּוֹלַעַת, בְּרִיָּתוֹ כִּשְׂעֹרָה, שֶׁהָיוּ מַרְאִים אוֹתוֹ עַל הָאֲבָנִים הָרְשׁוּמוֹת בַּדְּיוֹ וְהֵן נִבְקָעוֹת מֵאֵלֵיהֶן, וּבוֹ פִּתְּחוּ אַבְנֵי הָאֵפוֹד וְהַחֹשֶׁן, דִּכְתִיב בְּהוּ בְּמִלֻּאוֹתָם, שֶׁיִּהְיוּ שְׁלֵמוֹת שֶׁלֹּא יְחַסְּרוּ בָּהֶם, אֶלָּא כּוֹתֵב עֲלֵיהֶן בִּדְיוֹ וּמַרְאֶה לָהֶם שָׁמִיר עַל פְּנֵי הַדְּיוֹ בַּחוּץ:


השמיר – a kind of worm-like creature like the size of a barley-corn that they would show it–place it upon the stones marked with ink and they would split open on their own, [and through it] would open the stones of the Ephod and the Breastplate, as it is written concerning them (Exodus 28:20 – see also Talmud Sotah 48b): “in their mountings,” that they would be complete and not lacking [but he would write on them with ink] and the Shamir would show them outside upon the ink.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:12:3)


**וְנֹפֶת צוּפִים.** דְּבַשׁ הַבָּא מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ צִיפִיאָה, וּמְשֻׁבָּח מְאֹד:


ונופת צופים – honey that comes from a place whose name is the inner portion of the cells and is more praiseworthy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:12:4)


**וּפָסְקוּ אַנְשֵׁי אֲמָנָה.** אֲנָשִׁים שֶׁמַּאֲמִינִים בְּהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


ופסקו אנשי אמנה – that believe in the Holy One, blessed be He.



### Mishnah 13

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:13:1)


**הַטָּהֳרָה בִּטְּלָה אֶת הַטַּעַם.** טָהֳרָה שֶׁפָּסְקָה מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל, בִּטְלָהּ טַעַם הַפֵּרוֹת וְרֵיחָם. שֶׁבִּזְמַן שֶׁהָיוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל טְהוֹרִים וְעוֹסְקִים בַּטָּהֳרָה אַף הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא הָיָה מְטַהֵר אֶת הַפֵּרוֹת מִטַּעַם רַע וְרֵיחַ רַע:


הטהרה בטלה את הטעם – the ritual purity that ceased from Israel, the taste and aroma of the fruits were abolished, for at the time that Israel was ritually pure and engaged in ritual purity, even the Holy One, blessed be He would purify the produce from a bad taste and a bad smell.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:13:2)


**מַעֲשֵׂר בִּטֵל שֹׁמֶן דָּגָן.** דְּמַעֲשֵׂר אִקְּרֵי חֵלֶב, כְּדִכְתִיב (במדבר יח) כָּל חֵלֶב יִצְהָר:


מעשר בטל שומן דגן – for the tithe was called fat, as it is written (Numbers 18:12): “All the best of the oil.”



### Mishnah 14

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:14:1)


**בַּפֻּלְמוֹס שֶׁל אַסְפַּסְיָנוּס.** חַיִל שֶׁהֵבִיא אַסְפַּסְיָנוּס עַל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם:


בפולמוס של אספסיינוס – the army that Vespasian brought against Jerusalem.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:14:2)


**גָּזְרוּ עַל עַטְרוֹת חֲתָנִים.** הָיוּ עוֹשִׂים לַחֲתָנִים עֲטָרוֹת מֵאֶבֶן מֶלַח שֶׁהוּא צָלוּל כְּעֵין הַבְּדֹלַח וְצוֹבְעִין אוֹתָהּ בְּגָפְרִית כְּעֵין צִיּוּרִין שֶׁקּוֹרִין ניי"ל. וְאִית דִּמְפָרְשֵׁי עֲטָרוֹת שֶׁל הֲדַס וְשֶׁל וֶרֶד. וּשְׁנֵיהֶם אֲסוּרִים, וְאֵין מֻתָּר אֶלָּא עֲטָרוֹת שֶׁל קָנִים:


גזרו על עטרות חתנים – they would make garlands for the grooms from salt stone which is clear like the appearance of a jewel and they dye it with sulfur–bitumen like the appearance of embroidery which they call NEEL. And there are those who interpret–explain it as garlands of myrtle and of roses and both are forbidden, but only garlands of reeds are permitted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:14:3)


**וְעַל הָאֵרוּס.** עָשׂוּי כְּמִין נָפָה שֶׁדָּפְנוֹתֶיהָ עֲגֻלּוֹת וְשׁוֹטְחִים עַל פִּיהָ עוֹר דַּק וּמַכִּין עָלָיו בְּמַקֵּל דַּק וּמוֹצִיא קוֹל צָלוּל. וּבְלַעַ"ז קוֹרִין לוֹ טַנְבּוּרוֹ:


ועל האירוס – it is made like a winnow–sieve whose walls are round and they spread on its mouth a thin piece of leather and beat it with a thin mallet and it emits a clear sound and it the foreign language we call it TANBORO.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:14:4)


**עַטְרוֹת כַּלּוֹת.** כְּעֵין עִיר עֲשׂוּיָה מִזָּהָב:


עטרות כלות – similar to a city that is made from gold.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:14:5)


**וְשֶׁלֹּא יְלַמֵּד אָדָם אֶת בְּנוֹ יְוָנִית.** פֵּרוּשׁ, חָכְמָה יְוָנִית. וְהֵם רְמָזִים וְחִידוֹת שֶׁהָיוּ לַיְּוָנִים, וְלֹא הָיוּ מַכִּירִים בָּהֶם אֶלָּא הָרְגִילִים בָּהֶם. וּבַגְּמָרָא מְפָרֵשׁ טַעְמָא מִשּׁוּם מַעֲשֶׂה שֶׁהָיָה (כשיצאו) [כְּשֶׁצָּרוּ] מַלְכֵי חַשְׁמוֹנַאי זֶה עַל זֶה, הָיוּ אוֹתָן שֶׁבִּפְנִים מְשַׁלְשְׁלִים לְאוֹתָן שֶׁבַּחוּץ בְּכָל יוֹם דִּינָרִין בַּקֻּפָּה וְמַעֲלִין לָהֶן תְּמִידִין. הָיָה שָׁם זָקֵן אֶחָד, וְלָעַז לָהֶם בְּחָכְמָה יְוָנִית, כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהֵם עֲסוּקִין בָּעֲבוֹדָה אֵין נִמְסָרִין בְּיֶדְכֶם. אוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם שִׁלְשְׁלוּ לָהֶם דִּינָרִים בַּקֻּפָּה וְהֶעֱלוּ לָהֶם חֲזִיר. בְּאוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה אָמְרוּ אָרוּר אָדָם שֶׁיְּגַדֵּל חֲזִיר, וְאָרוּר אָדָם שֶׁיְּלַמֵּד לִבְנוֹ חָכְמָה יְוָנִית:


ושלא ילמד אדם את בנו יונית – the explanation is Greek wisdom and there are hints and riddles that the Greeks had and only those who were accustomed to them would know them, and in the Gemara (Talmud Sotah 49b) it explains the reason because of an incident when the Hasmonean kings fought one another (the allusion is to the struggle between the two sons of Alexander Jannaeus, Hyrcanus and Aristobulus; Hyrcanus had the assistance of the Romans who besieged Jerusalem). Those who were inside would let down to those who were outside denarii (i.e., money) in a basket and haul up for them [animals for] the daily offerings. There was one elder there who was learned in Greek wisdom, speaking with them in Greek: As long as you are engaged in the Temple service, they will never surrender to you. That same day, they let down denarii in the basket and hauled up for them a pig. At that time, they said: “Cursed be the person who raises a pig and cursed be the man who teaches his son Greek wisdom.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:14:6)


**בַּפֻּלְמוֹס הָאַחֲרוֹן.** הוּא חֻרְבַּן הַבַּיִת. וְשֶׁל טִיטוּס נַמִּי הֲוָה:


בפולמוס האחרון – this is the destruction of the [Second] Temple and it was of Titus.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:14:7)


**בָּאַפִּרְיוֹן.** כְּמוֹ אֹהֶל שֶׁל מְעִילִין וְטַלִּיתוֹת מֻזְהָבוֹת:


באפריון – like a tent of golden robes and cloaks.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:14:8)


**וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ הִתִּירוּ שֶׁתֵּצֵא כַּלָּה בָּאַפִּרְיוֹן.** מִשּׁוּם צְנִיעוּתָא. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


ורבותינו התירו שתצא כלה באפריון – because of modesty and such is the Halakha.



### Mishnah 15

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:15:1)


**בָּטְלוּ מוֹשְׁלֵי מְשָׁלִים.** כְּגוֹן מִמְּשָׁלוֹת שׁוּעָלִים, דְּאָמְרִינַן בְּסַנְהֶדְרִין [לח:] כִּי הָוֵי דָרִישׁ רַבִּי מֵאִיר בְּפִרְקָא הֲוָה דָרִישׁ תִּלְתָא שְׁמַעְתָּתָא וְתִלְתָא אַגַּדְתָּא וְתִלְתָא מַתְלֵי:


בטלו מושלי משלים – such as the parables of the foxes that we state in [Tractate] Sanhedrin [38b] when Rabbi Meir would expound on the lesson and would divide his exposition into three parts: one-third devoted to legal discussion, one-third to homily and one-third to parables.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:15:2)


**הַשַּׁקְדָּנִים.** שׁוֹקְדִים עַל דַּלְתוֹת בֵּית הַמִּדְרָשׁ לַיְלָה וָיוֹם, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בִּיבָמוֹת [סג:] דְּאָמַר בֶּן עַזַּאי מָה אֶעֱשֶׂה וְנַפְשִׁי חָשְׁקָה בַּתּוֹרָה:


השקדנים – they are attendant on the doors of the Academy–Bet Midrash night and day as we state in [Tractate] Yevamot [63b] as Ben Azzai stated: What can I do when my soul is attached to–in love with the Torah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:15:3)


**הַדַּרְשָׁנִים.** שֶׁהָיָה בָּקִי בְּמִדְרַשׁ טַעֲמֵי מִקְרָאוֹת, כִּדְאָמַר [בְּרָכוֹת יב:] וְלֹא זָכִיתִי שֶׁתֵּאָמֵר יְצִיאַת מִצְרַיִם בַּלֵּילוֹת עַד שֶׁדְּרָשָׁהּ בֶּן זוֹמָא:


הדרשנים – he was expert in Midrash and the rationales of the Biblical verses, as it states [Talmud Berakhot 12b – actually Tractate Berakhot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 5]: “And I did not succeed (in proving) that the Exodus from Egypt should be mentioned at night until [I heard] the exposition of Ben Zoma.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:15:4)


**בָּטַל כְּבוֹד תּוֹרָה.** שֶׁהָיָה נוֹתֵן לֵב לִדְרֹשׁ עַל כָּל קוֹץ וְקוֹץ [מְנָחוֹת כח:]. וְזֶהוּ כְּבוֹד תּוֹרָה גָּדוֹל, שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ דָּבָר לְבַטָּלָה:


בטל כבוד תורה – He would give attention to expound upon each and every stroke on letter [Tractate Menahot 29b]. This is the great honor of the Torah which has nothing to abolish it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:15:5)


**בָּטְלוּ אַנְשֵׁי מַעֲשֶׂה.** שֶׁעוֹשִׂים מַעֲשִׂים מֻפְלָאִים כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בְּתַעֲנִיּוֹת [כה.] יֹאמַר לַחֹמֶץ וְיִדְלַק, וְלָעִזִּים לְהָבִיא זְאֵבִים בְּקַרְנֵיהֶם:


שבטלו אנשי מעשה – they do wondrous deeds as we state in [Tractate] Taanit [25a]: he will ordain that vinegar should burn, and to goats that they should bring wolves on their horns.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:15:6)


**קַטְנוּתָם שֶׁל חֲסִידִים.** שֶׁהָיוּ הַחֲסִידִים הוֹלְכִים וּמִתְקַטְנִין, וְהוּא הָיָה מִקְּטַנֵיהֶן וְסוֹפָן:


קטנותם של חסידים – they wee the pious individuals walking and becoming scarce and he was from their most inferior and their end.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:15:7)


**מִשֶּׁמֵּת רַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל הַזָּקֵן בָּטַל כְּבוֹד הַתּוֹרָה.** שֶׁעַד שֶׁמֵּת הוּא, הָיָה בְּרִיאוּת בָּעוֹלָם, וְהָיוּ לוֹמְדִים תּוֹרָה מְעֻמָּד [מְגִלָּה כא.], וּמִשֶּׁמֵּת בָּאת תְּשׁוּת כֹּחַ לָעוֹלָם וְהִתְחִילוּ לִלְמֹד מְיֻשָּׁב. וְזֶהוּ בָּטַל כְּבוֹד תּוֹרָה:


משמת רבן גמליאל הזקן בטל כבוד התורה – and until he died [Tractate Megillah 21a], there was health in the world, and hey would study the Torah while standing. And when he died, feebleness of strength came to the world and they began to study while sitting, and this is the abolishment of the honor of the Torah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sotah 9:15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sotah/r/9:15:8)


**מִשֶּׁמֵּת רַבִּי בָּטְלָה עֲנָוָה.** תַּלְמִידָיו שֶׁל רַבִּי הוֹסִיפוּ וְכָתְבוּ זֶה בַּמִּשְׁנָה:


משמת רבי בטלה ענוה – the students of Rebbe (i.e., Rabbi Judah the Patriarch) added and wrote this in the Mishnah.
